### [1] [Form factors of field exponentials in the 1+1 dimensional Sine-Gordon model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14365)
*Karol K. Kozlowski,Alex Simon*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在1+1维Sine-Gordon量子场论中，给出了Fock空间所有扇区（孤子、反孤子、呼吸子）中广义积分核（表示场算符的指数）的基本构建块——形状因子的显式表达式，并复现了文献中先前通过自由场方法获得的多种结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Sine-Gordon量子场论中，场算符指数的形式因子是关键的量，但此前缺乏在所有Fock空间扇区中的统一显式表达式。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供系统且完整的构建块表达。

Method: 作者推导了Sine-Gordon量子场论中广义积分核的形状因子在这些形式因子在所有Fock空间扇区（包括孤子、反孤子和呼吸子扇区）的显式解析表达式，并验证这些表达式能够复现先前文献中通过自由场方法获得的结果。

Result: 获得了一套完整且统一的形状因子显式公式，覆盖所有Fock空间扇区，并与过往文献中的自由场方法结果相互印证。

Conclusion: 该工作为Sine-Gordon理论中形状因子的计算提供了一个统一且完备的框架，可作为后续研究的基础工具。

Abstract: This work provides explicit expressions, in all sectors of the Fock space: soliton, antisoliton and breather, for the fundamental building blocks -the form factors-
  of the generalised integral kernels which represent the exponential of the field operator in the 1+1 dimensional Sine-Gordon quantum field theory. The obtained form factors reproduce the various results obtained earlier in the literature using, in particular, the free field approach.

</details>


### [2] [A semiclassical limit of reduced Hartree-Fock theory at positive temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14436)
*Dominic Shillingford*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了约化Hartree-Fock巨势在正温度下向Thomas-Fermi巨势的半经典极限收敛问题，提出了极限泛函的密度-势对偶表示，并证明了该对偶问题无对偶间隙且存在极大值点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约化Hartree-Fock巨势在正温度下的半经典极限理论已有初步结论，但极限泛函的显式表示及其数学性质（如对偶性、极大值存在性）尚未得到充分刻画。本文旨在为此提供严格的数学框架，并统一处理一般费米子熵函数类。

Method: 通过构造密度-势对偶表示来刻画极限泛函；利用凸分析和对偶理论证明该对偶问题无对偶间隙且极大值存在；通过建立均匀半经典迹公式（对满足公共约束条件的局部预紧势族一致成立）来完成极限的证明；最后通过对Fermi-Dirac熵的特例验证与标准正温度Thomas-Fermi自由能泛函的一致性，并取温度趋于零验证与零温巨势的收敛关系。

Result: 建立了极限Thomas-Fermi巨势的密度-势对偶表示；证明了对偶问题无对偶间隙并存在极大值；证明了所得巨势在温度趋于零时收敛于已知的零温巨势；对Fermi-Dirac熵情形，结果与标准正温度Thomas-Fermi自由能泛函一致；证明了一个对局部预紧势族一致成立的半经典迹公式。

Conclusion: 本文为约化Hartree-Fock巨势的半经典极限提供了完整的泛函分析刻画，其核心贡献在于极限泛函的对偶表示与均匀半经典迹公式。

Abstract: It is established that the reduced Hartree-Fock grand potential at positive temperature converges in the semiclassical limit to a Thomas-Fermi grand potential. In particular, a density-potential dual representation of the limiting functional is proposed for a general class of fermionic entropy functions. The corresponding dual problem has no duality gap and admits a maximizer. The resulting grand potential converges to the established zero-temperature grand potential as the temperature tends to zero and, for the Fermi-Dirac entropy, agrees with the standard positive-temperature Thomas-Fermi free-energy functional. The proof also establishes a semiclassical trace formula that is uniform over locally precompact families of potentials subject to a common confinement condition.

</details>


### [3] [The Okinawa Lectures on Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14523)
*Klaas Landsman*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于经典与量子熵的综述课程论文，系统介绍经典熵（大偏差理论视角）和量子熵（基于von Neumann代数、模理论和Haagerup非交换L^p空间），涵盖Sanov、Cramér、Gärtner-Ellis、Varadhan定理，Kullback-Leibler熵、Umegaki熵、Rényi熵、Araki-Uhlmann相对熵以及Connes-Stormer-Narnhofer-Thirring熵等，旨在为理论物理学家提供严格数学框架下的熵理论导论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熵是统计物理、信息论和量子力学中的核心概念，但经典熵与量子熵之间在数学结构和深层联系上缺乏系统梳理。本文受黑洞热力学启发，旨在以数学严格性与理论物理可读性兼顾的方式，统一呈现经典与量子熵在概率论框架下的完整图景，特别阐明量子熵如何借助von Neumann代数和模理论在数学上自洽地定义与计算。

Method: 本文采用课程式的系统讲解方法：首先在经典概率论中引入大偏差理论（Sanov、Cramér、Gärtner-Ellis、Varadhan定理）来刻画经典熵；然后通过统计假设检验的关键角色建立经典相对熵（Kullback-Leibler熵）与量子相对熵（Umegaki熵、Rényi变形）之间的联系；对量子情形，将系统状态形式化为von Neumann代数，引入Tomita-Takesaki模理论定义Araki-Uhlmann相对熵，并利用Haagerup非交换L^p空间将其表示为密度算子和迹的形式；最后以von Neumann代数为框架定义Connes-Stormer-Narnhofer-Thirring熵作为Kolmogorov-Sinai熵的量子推广。

Result: 本文成功构建了从经典到量子熵的统一数学框架，展示了所有经典熵（KL熵、Rényi熵）均可通过大偏差理论与统计假设检验自然导出，量子熵（Umegaki熵、Araki-Uhlmann相对熵）作为其量子对应物在von Neumann代数框架下严格定义，并通过Haagerup L^p空间实现可计算性。CSNT熵为动力系统与遍历理论中的Kolmogorov-Sinai熵提供了量子推广。

Conclusion: 经典与量子熵虽在公式层面相似，但深层联系在于统计假设检验中的核心作用。量子熵的严格定义需要von Neumann代数、Tomita-Takesaki理论和Haagerup L^p空间等现代数学工具，这为理论物理（特别是黑洞热力学）中的熵概念提供了坚实的数学基础。课程的先修要求为本科概率论、泛函分析和量子理论。

Abstract: After a historical introduction, the most important classical and quantum entropies are introduced as constructions in classical and quantum probability theory. Classical entropies are studied from large deviation theory, including theorems of Sanov, Cramér, Gärtner-Ellis, and Varadhan, and are illustrated in some applications to both Boltzmannian and Gibbsian statistical physics. Quantum entropies superficially connect to classical entropies at the formula level, but more deeply do so via the crucial role of entropy in statistical hypothesis testing. The classical (relative) Kullback-Leibler entropy, its quantum counterpart introduced by Umegaki, as well as their deformations proposed by Renyi all fit naturally in this context. Quantum entropy faces the new problem of defining and computing the relative entropy of a pair of states on a subsystem, here formalized as a von Neumann algebra. This requires modular (aka Tomita-Takesaki) theory, which provides the framework for the relative quantum entropies introduced by Araki and Uhlmann (these encompass both the Kullback-Leibler and Umegaki entropies as special cases). To (re)define and compute these entropies in terms of density operators and traces, further constructions are needed, namely Haagerup's noncommutative L^p spaces. Von Neumann algebras also provide the setting for the Connes-Stormer-Narnhofer-Thirring entropy, which is a quantum version of the Kolmogorov-Sinai entropy in dynamical systems and ergodic theory, to which we also provide an introduction. This course was originally inspired by, and should be relevant to, black hole thermodynamics, although we discuss neither this application nor the second law. The course tries to be both mathematically rigorous and interesting to theoretical physicists. Prerequisites are undergraduate probability theory, functional analysis, and quantum theory.

</details>

### [4] [Homological Mirror Symmetry for Affine Log Calabi-Yau Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13779)
*Umut Varolgunes*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文为光滑复仿射log Calabi-Yau曲面U的完全有限型Liouville流形$\widehat{U}$提供一个算法，构造有限型拟射影Z-概形$U^\vee$，使得其导出范畴与$U^\vee$的凝聚层导出范畴（在任意域上）等价，即证明了该情形的同调镜像对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于log Calabi-Yau曲面的镜像对称，已有的Hacking-Keating定理依赖于找到合适的几乎环面模型（almost toric model）。作者旨在系统性地构造这种模型，并证明它确实与原始的$\widehat{U}$保持分级的强精确辛同构，从而将镜像对称的结果从特定例子推广到一般光滑复仿射log Calabi-Yau曲面。

Method: 作者首先通过精确特征射线图（exact eigenray diagram）编码构造一个几乎环面模型；然后利用symplectic Torelli定理（适用于symplectic log Calabi-Yau对）证明该模型与$\widehat{U}$之间存在保持分级的强精确辛同构；最后调用Hacking-Keating的同调镜像对称定理得出结论。

Result: 给出了一个算法，对任意的光滑复仿射log Calabi-Yau曲面构造出有限型拟射影Z-概形$U^\vee$，并证明了在任意域$\Bbbk$上$D^\pi(\mathcal{W}(\widehat{U},\Bbbk)) \cong D^b\operatorname{Coh}(U^\vee_{\Bbbk})$成立。

Conclusion: 证明了任意光滑复仿射log Calabi-Yau曲面都存在同调镜像对称的实现，主要创新在于将几乎环面模型的构造系统化，并通过symplectic Torelli定理验证其与原始流形的等价性。

Abstract: Let $U$ be a smooth complex affine log Calabi--Yau surface, and let $\widehat{U}$ be its complete finite-type Liouville manifold, equipped with its natural grading structure. We give an algorithm that constructs a finite-type quasi-projective $\mathbb{Z}$-scheme $U^\vee$ such that \[ D^π\bigl(\mathcal{W}(\widehat{U},\Bbbk)\bigr) \cong D^b\operatorname{Coh}(U^\vee_{\Bbbk}) \] for every field $\Bbbk$. Our main contribution is to construct an almost toric model encoded by an exact eigenray diagram and, using the symplectic Torelli theorem for symplectic log Calabi--Yau pairs, to prove that this model is grading-preserving strongly exact symplectomorphic to $\widehat{U}$. The result then follows from the homological mirror symmetry theorem of Hacking--Keating.

</details>

### [5] [Quasinormal modes as exterior probes of black hole interiors in our Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13643)
*Sašo Grozdanov,Vita Movrin,Samuel Valach*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究封闭在反射性类时腔体中的黑洞在受线性扰动时的拟正则模谱。结合arXiv:2606.11297，作者建立了热积公式并推导了高频拟正则泛音的统一渐近表达式，适用于渐近平坦、de Sitter和Anti-de Sitter时空。该谱特征编码了黑洞内部及奇点的信息，原理上可被事件视界外的观测者获取。作者随后提出一种观测方案，利用吸积等离子体作为部分透射镜，对于小型原初黑洞，或可从射电波段电磁波中提取黑洞内部的具体信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞内部及其奇点的信息通常被认为无法被外部观测者直接获取（事件视界的存在）。作者旨在寻找一种哪怕在原理上能从外部提取黑洞内部信息的谱学信号，并探索切实可行的观测途径。

Method: 通过将黑洞置于反射性类时腔体中并施加线性扰动，分析其重力、电磁及其他扰动的拟正则模谱；结合先前的arXiv:2606.11297工作建立热积公式，并推导高频拟正则泛音的统一渐近表达式。在观测层面，提出以吸积等离子体作为部分透射镜的方案，针对小质量原初黑洞在射电波段进行观测。

Result: 所建立的热积公式和统一渐近表达式在渐近平坦、de Sitter和Anti-de Sitter三种时空背景下均成立。研究显示拟正则模谱可直接编码黑洞内部及奇点信息，且理论上可为外部观测者所获取。提出的吸积等离子体观测方案表明，对于小型原初黑洞，有望从射电波段的电磁波中提取黑洞内部的定量信息。

Conclusion: 作者认为黑洞拟正则模谱可作为探针，使外部观测者在原理上能够提取黑洞内部及奇点的信息，并通过吸积等离子体作为部分透射镜的观测方案，在小质量原初黑洞的射电观测中实现这一目标，为探测黑洞内部结构提供了新的理论框架和实验途径。

Abstract: We consider a scenario in which a black hole is enclosed in a reflecting timelike cavity and linearly perturbed. For the resulting quasinormal spectra of gravitational, electromagnetic, and other perturbations, in conjunction with our arXiv:2606.11297, we establish a thermal product formula and derive a universal asymptotic expression for high quasinormal overtones. Both results hold in asymptotically flat, de Sitter, and Anti-de Sitter spacetimes (known previously). This spectral signature directly encodes information about the black hole interior and its singularity, and is, at least in principle, accessible to an observer outside the event horizon. We then propose a concrete observational setup in which accreting plasma plays the role of a partially transmissive mirror. For small, hypothetical, primordial black holes, we argue that one may thereby be able to extract concrete information about the black hole interior from electromagnetic waves in the radiowave spectrum.

</details>


### [6] [Lee-Yang paradigm of phase transition in eigenstate thermalized systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13174)
*Yongjiang Xu,Weixin Sun,Chushun Tian,Huajia Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文展示了在全息共形场论中，本征态热化系统的自关联函数可以映射为一个虚拟相互作用瞬子气体的配分函数，并在中心荷c→∞极限下表现出Lee-Yang范式的热力学类相变，相变点位于著名的禁戒奇点处。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孤立量子系统中的相变现象一直以来难以实现，作者旨在探索在本征态热化系统中如何出现类似热力学相变的行为，并揭示其与Lee-Yang范式的联系。

Method: 作者将全息共形场论中本征态的自关联函数期望值映射为虚拟相互作用瞬子气体的配分函数Z_gauge(z)，其中虚时间的共形变换为z=1-e^{-τ}，中心荷c分别模拟瞬子的逸度与体积。通过分析c→∞极限下配分函数零点的分布来研究相变行为。

Result: 研究发现，在c→∞极限下，一对复共轭零点移动到实轴上，恰好位于著名的禁戒奇点处。系统穿过该奇点时会从低逸度相转变为高逸度相，伴随自由能标度行为和主导微观构型的显著变化。

Conclusion: 本征态热化系统中存在丰富而深刻的相变物理，其相变机制遵循Lee-Yang理论范式，说明即使看似热化的量子系统也能展现非平庸的相变行为。

Abstract: As phase transitions in isolated quantum systems remain elusive, here we show how a thermodynamic-like phase transition, falling into the Lee-Yang paradigm, can arise in systems displaying eigenstate thermalization. Specifically, we show that in holographic conformal field theories, the eigenstate expectation of the auto-correlation function can be mapped to the partition function ${\cal Z}_{gauge}(z)$ of a virtual interacting instanton gas, with the conformal mapping of the imaginary time: $z=1-e^{-τ}$ and the central charge $c$ mimicking the instanton fugacity and volume, respectively. We find that akin to the Lee-Yang paradigm, for $c\to\infty$ a pair of complex conjugate zeros of ${\cal Z}_{gauge}(z)$ move to the real axis located at the famous forbidden singularity. Passing through the singularity the system transits from the low- to high-fugacity phase, accompanied by dramatic changes in scaling behaviors of the free energy and dominant microscopic configurations. Our findings indicate that physics of phase transitions from eigenstate thermalization is very rich.

</details>


### [7] [Localizing Momentum Waves on Mazes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13647)
*Tobi Ramella,Nicholas P. Warner*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过计算纯M5膜上的动量波简单实例，检验了arXiv:2404.14477中提出的将动量波置于M2-M5交叉膜迷宫上的方案。作者重新分析了近膜M5-P系统的BPS方程，证明该线性系统能捕获所有零动量波，并发现动量波仍局域在AdS3因子上（反映M2-M5-P结构），表现为奇异源和光滑局域凸函数两种形式，支持了独立动量波可在每个膜交叉处局域化的更广泛提议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证arXiv:2404.14477提出的动量波放置在M2-M5相交膜迷宫上的方案的合理性。通过研究更简单的纯M5-P系统，检查其BPS方程线性化是否完整捕获所有零动量波，以及超对称规定是否确实反映了M2-M5-P结构，从而为该更广泛提议提供支持性证据。

Method: 作者计算了一系列纯M5膜上的动量波（M5-P系统）简单实例，重新分析了具有完整M2-M5-P系统规定超对称结构的近膜BPS方程，并验证arXiv:2404.14477中获得的线性系统能否捕获所有零动量波。通过分析动量波在AdS7中AdS3因子上的局域化行为，区分了显式奇异源和平滑局域凸函数（反映"动量迁移"）两种局域化形式。

Result: （1）arXiv:2404.14477中得到的线性系统能够完整捕获纯M5-P系统上的所有零动量波；（2）尽管实例中没有显式M2膜，动量波仍局域在AdS3因子上，表明超对称规定确实反映M2-M5-P结构；（3）动量局域化呈现两种形式：显式奇异源和代表"动量迁移"的光滑局域凸函数；（4）这些结果支持了arXiv:2404.14477中"独立动量波可在每个膜交叉处局域化"的更广泛提议。

Conclusion: 纯M5-P系统的简单实例验证了arXiv:2404.14477提出的动量波方案的正确性和普适性。动量波在无M2膜的情况下仍局域在AdS3因子上，证明了超对称结构规定的有效性。作者建议进一步推广膜交叉处的动量波，这可能对全息对偶有重要影响。

Abstract: We examine the proposal and analysis of arXiv:2404.14477 for putting momentum waves on mazes of intersecting M2 and M5 branes by computing a family of simple examples of momentum waves on pure M5 branes. We re-analyze the BPS equations for the near-brane M5-P system with the prescribed supersymmetry structure of the full M2-M5-P system and show that the general linear system obtained in arXiv:2404.14477 captures all of the null momentum waves on the simpler M5-P system. Despite the absence of explicit M2 branes in our examples, we find that the momentum waves still localize on an AdS$_3$ factor of the AdS$_7$ associated with M5 branes, showing that the supersymmetry prescription still reflects the M2-M5-P structure. This momentum localization takes two forms: explicit singular sources and smooth, localized bump functions reflecting ''momentum migration'' that is a feature of other microstate geometries. The simple, physical form of the momentum localization in our examples supports the broader proposal in arXiv:2404.14477 that independent momentum waves can localize at each brane intersection. We suggest further generalizations of momentum waves at brane intersections that could have important implications in holography.

</details>


### [8] [Large lumps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14384)
*D. Bazeia,I. Bezerra,M. A. Marques,R. Menezes*

In the large-$k$ WKB limit, the saddle that controls an $individual$ primary is the BTZ black hole with a $singular$ horizon. We study the (grand-)canonical partition function of the microstates with density of states dictated by modular invariance. The temperature and chemical potential then fix the holonomy saddle to be the Euclidean BTZ black hole with a $smoothly$ contractible thermal cycle. A density of heavy primaries growing as $e^{2πγQP}$ would instead lead to a defect (or excess) saddle, except at the Cardy value $γ=1$ fixed by modular invariance. After analytic continuation, the eternal Lorentzian black hole with a smooth horizon is therefore not just any thermal ensemble, but the ``modular ensemble" of (semi-classically singular) horizonless microstates. We compute the thermal fluctuations in this ensemble, and reproduce the fluctuations in the area of the form expected from semi-classical general relativity. Ensembles of theories do not play a direct role in any of our discussions. We also note that these microstates, unlike fuzzballs, do $not$ break the isometries of the black hole.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Goldberg在1973年证明了对于除S^2和RP^2以外的闭曲面X，strand map ι_*的核等于嵌入圆盘中支撑的纯辫的正常闭包。这一经典描述是否适用于更一般的拓扑空间是一个自然且重要的开放问题，需要在任意有限连通单纯复形上推广该定理。

Method: 核心工具包括两个：(1) 在X的某一点对配置空间进行graph-of-spaces分解；(2) 一个resolution过程，将任意复形约化为简单模型。此外引入"可容许树"（admissible tree）的概念——即scaffold的极大树，且与自由部分附着的边界类型和内部类型相容，作为刻画Goldberg性质的关键组合条件。

Result: 得到了以下主要分类定理：strand map满射当且仅当X不拓扑同构于S^1；X是弱Goldberg的当且仅当X的自由部分是森林；X是Goldberg的当且仅当X容许一个可容许树。此外还完整分类了核为平凡群的情形，解决了S^2和RP^2这两个Goldberg原始定理中的例外曲面，并对流形和图给出了完备的答案。

Conclusion: 该工作将Goldberg关于闭曲面的经典定理完整推广到任意有限连通单纯复形，以纯组合条件（自由部分为森林、可容许树的存在性）精确刻画了复形的Goldberg性质，并解决了所有例外情形，为该方向的后续研究提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: For a finite connected simplicial complex $X$, the strand map $ι_\ast$, from $\mathbb{P}_n(X)$ to $\prod_{i=1}^nπ_1(X,x_i^0)$, sends a pure braid to the homotopy classes of its strands. A theorem of Goldberg (1973) computes its kernel when $X$ is a closed surface other than $S^2$ and $\mathbb{RP}^2$: the kernel is the normal closure of the pure braids supported in an embedded disc. We extend this picture to arbitrary finite connected simplicial complexes. Call $X$ $\textit{weakly Goldberg}$ if some contractible subcomplex $X_0\subseteq X$ realises Goldberg's description, $\kerι_\ast=\left\langle \operatorname{im}(\mathbb{P}_n(X_0)\to\mathbb{P}_n(X)) \right\rangle$, and $\textit{Goldberg}$ if $X_0$ can moreover be chosen so that $\mathbb{P}_n(X_0)\to\mathbb{P}_n(X)$ is injective. We prove that the strand map is surjective if and only if $X\not\cong S^1$; that $X$ is weakly Goldberg if and only if its free part is a forest; and that $X$ is Goldberg if and only if it admits an $\textit{admissible tree}$ -- a maximal tree of a scaffold, compatible with the boundary and interior types of the attachments of the free part to the thick components. We also classify the complexes for which the kernel is trivial, settle the exceptional surfaces $S^2$ and $\mathbb{RP}^2$, and obtain complete answers for manifolds and for graphs. The main tools are a graph-of-spaces decomposition of the configuration space at a point of $X$ and a resolution procedure reducing an arbitrary complex to a simple model.

</details>

Conclusion: 本文建立了统一处理Galois作用在等变谱与模形式上的理论框架，不仅整合了经典已知结果，还推广到更广泛的情形，并为等变环谱的完美模范畴提供了一种结构性分解工具，由此导出了局部化不变量的Mayer-Vietoris序列这一有价值的推论。

Abstract: In this article, the cyclotomic Galois action on topological K-theory adjoined with a primitive $n$th root of unity and the famous $GL_1(\mathbf{Z}/n)$- and $GL_2(\mathbf{Z}/n)$-Galois actions on topological modular forms with $Γ_1(n)$- and $Γ(n)$-level structures are unified and generalised. This is done by defining a quotient stack in derived algebraic geometry parametrising constant finite abelian subgroups of $\mathbf{P}$-divisible groups, and studying how these quotient stacks and various notions of torsors interact with Galois extensions formed by taking algebraic and categorical invariants. Taking global sections then yields the titular Galois extensions on $K$-geometric fixed points, recovering and refining the well-known examples above and providing new ones.

Compatibility with the non-standard affine twist fixes the scalars needed to construct a natural isomorphism on projectives, and uniqueness of right exact extension then gives \[

Abstract: Parameter estimation is a fundamental challenge in the calibration of ordinary differential equation (ODE) models, where repeated numerical integration can lead to high computational cost. In this work, we investigate whether quantum algorithms can be leveraged to assist parameter estimation in nonlinear dynamical systems. We develop a hybrid classical-quantum framework that reformulates a data-assimilation-augmented parameter estimation problem as a combinatorial optimization task. Model dynamics and data assimilation are enforced entirely on the classical side, while the resulting parameter estimation cost functional is discretized and approximated by a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) surrogate. This surrogate is mapped to an Ising Hamiltonian, and quantum optimizers are used to search for low-energy configurations corresponding to candidate parameter estimates. We apply the framework to SIS and SIR epidemic models, the chaotic Lorenz-63 system, and a high-dimensional two-layer Lorenz-96 system. In this setting, the method is used to recover classical system parameters from partial state observations across steady-state, chaotic, and high-dimensional multiscale dynamical systems. Numerical experiments with synthetic data show that the proposed approach accurately recovers parameters while requiring data-assimilation solves only on a prescribed coarse grid. The framework avoids quantum state tomography, illustrating a viable pathway for integrating quantum optimization into data-driven parameter estimation for nonlinear dynamical systems.

Abstract: High-fidelity readout with strong Purcell protection of qubit coherence is essential for scalable superconducting quantum processors, yet the finite passband and sizable footprint of conventional band-pass Purcell filters make them hard to scale. Here we introduce a scalable edge-pass Purcell filter that separates the readout band from the protected qubit band by a single transmission edge, freeing the readout resonators from bandwidth constraint. Depending on whether the transmitting band lies above or below the cutoff, the compact network is realized as a high-pass filter (HPF) or a low-pass filter (LPF). The HPF reaches an average readout fidelity of 99.46(4)% (up to 99.56%) with a 150-ns pulse, and the LPF reaches 99.49(3)% (up to 99.57%) with a 130-ns pulse. Here, we numerically study the tradeoff between depolarizing noise, entanglement as quantified by the bipartite negativity measure, and HOP in quantum volume circuits. Abstract: A projective measurement of a many-body state produces a microscopic snapshot, usually viewed as random classical data. We show that partial occupation snapshots of the critical XX chain contain a universal angle with a precise conformal meaning. Dividing the ring into two measured and two unmeasured arcs, we assign geometry-dependent conformal side weights to the observed occupations and obtain a compact variable $δ_L$. At every finite size, $δ_L$ is fixed by the measured sites alone; the particular complete-configuration lift $X_L$ used in the proof additionally depends on unobserved particles. This angle is an exact microscopic compact coordinate whose scaling-limit law is that of the relative Dirichlet phase of the associated conformal quadrilateral---the boundary coordinate conjugate to charge in continuum post-measurement descriptions. Exact free-fermion determinants yield all of its Fourier moments. We prove that the lift becomes Gaussian with variance $2h(ζ)$, where $h(ζ)$ is the rectangle modulus, and hence $\langle e^{\ii qδ_L}\rangle\to e^{-h(ζ)q^2}$. Thus raw quantum snapshots realize the heat kernel on a circle and provide an outcome-level microscopic foundation for the compact zero-mode sector of Born averages over fluctuating conformal boundary conditions.

Abstract: Absolutely maximally entangled states require every reduction of at most half of the parties to be maximally mixed, a condition that is both rigid and often impossible for qubit systems. Previous work introduced the quantum extremal number, which maximizes the number of exactly maximally mixed half-body marginals, and determined the exact value Qex(8,4)=56. The present work develops a robust extension of this extremal problem. For a subsystem $A$, the marginal maximal-mixing defect is defined by \[ D_A=2^{|A|}\operatorname{Tr}(ρ_A^2)-1 =2^{|A|}\left\|ρ_A-\frac{I_A}{2^{|A|}}\right\|_2^2, \] and $Q_{\mathrm{ex},\varepsilon}^{D}(n,k)$ is defined as the maximum number of $k$-body marginals satisfying $D_A\leq\varepsilon$ in an $n$-qubit pure state. This counting problem differs from approximate $k$-uniformity, which requires all $k$-body marginals to obey a common error bound.

Combined with the known exact eight-qubit construction, this yields the stability plateau \[ Q_{\mathrm{ex},\varepsilon}^{D}(8,4)=56, \qquad 0\leq\varepsilon<\frac15. \] For odd systems of $2k+1$ qubits, the exact forbidden hypergraph $H_k$ is used to derive explicit finite-error stability radii. In particular, $Q_{\mathrm{ex},\varepsilon}^{D}(9,4)\leq120$ for $0\leq\varepsilon<1/17$. These results turn exact quantum Turán obstructions into quantitative robustness statements and identify intervals on which quantum extremal numbers are stable under imperfect marginal mixedness.

</details>

Abstract: For testing quantum mechanics in the macroscopic domain, a major challenge is to devise effective means for enhancing the observable nonclassical signatures despite the ubiquitous presence of environmental decoherence. Toward this goal, we invoke the Quantum Zeno Effect (QZE) for achieving a tunable amplification of an inherently nonclassical quantum disturbance induced by any measurement. Such an enhancement of otherwise small and decoherence-suppressed nonclassicality can arise from the cumulative quantum disturbances generated by repetitive measurements, with the tunability of amplification controlled by the number of measurements. To evidence this, we formulate a testable loophole-free scheme using a massive oscillator, where the system preparation requires trapping and ground-state cooling of a massive object. The required measurements can be realized through a beam-splitter-type interaction between the mechanical oscillator and an optical field, followed by photon detection. Abstract: To maximize the potential of quantum error-correcting codes, it is essential to develop high-performance decoders. The subsystem many-hypercube (MHC) codes have been developed to achieve both high encoding rates and low-weight syndrome-measurements, but the introduction of gauge degrees of freedom makes decoding more challenging. In this work, we develop neural-network-based decoders for the subsystem MHC codes in a circuit-level noise model. We further show that recurrent neural decoders outperform simple fully connected neural decoders, and can decode syndrome-measurement sequences longer than those used during training.

Abstract: Entanglement certification is often performed on states that have already undergone noisy transmission or processing. Governed by a sublattice selection rule, the coupling activates exclusively in intersecting configurations and decays exponentially with distance. Abstract: Simulating strongly correlated quantum materials often involves not a single Hamiltonian, but a family of Hamiltonians whose ground states evolve across experimentally tunable couplings. We introduce the Foundation Neural Effective Hamiltonian (FNEH), which projects a Hamiltonian family onto a compact subspace spanned by FNQS sampled at selected couplings. By variationally combining FNQS across parameter space, FNEH systematically improves their ground-state approximation and can recover phase boundaries that the foundation model misidentifies. Once the required operator matrix elements are sampled, FNEH enables sweeps over couplings, observables, and phase boundaries at a cost governed by the small effective-Hamiltonian dimension, without repeated neural-network sampling at every target coupling. We demonstrate FNEH in strongly correlated moiré materials, where it accurately resolves competing phases, enables high-resolution multidimensional phase scans, and substantially reduces the computational cost of exploring many target Hamiltonians. The results open a new avenue for studying strongly correlated quantum materials with foundation models.

Abstract: Quantum circuits are fundamental components of quantum computing, and state-vector-based quantum circuit simulation is a widely used technique for tracking qubit behavior throughout circuit evolution. However, simulating a circuit with $n$ qubits requires $2^{n+4}$ bytes of memory, making simulations of more than 40 qubits feasible only on supercomputers. To address this limitation, we propose the Qu-Trefoil, a system designed for large-scale quantum circuit simulations on an FPGA-based platform called Trefoil. Trefoil is a multi-FPGA system connected to eight storage subsystems, each equipped with 32 SATA disks. Qu-Trefoil integrates a suite of HLS-based universal quantum gates, including Clifford gates (Hadamard (H), Pauli-Z (Z), Phase (S), Controlled-NOT (CNOT)), the T gate, and unitary matrix computation, along with HDL-designed modules for system-wide integration. This achievement represents a significant milestone in the advancement of quantum computing simulations. Furthermore, thanks to its unique architecture, Qu-Trefoil is more accessible, flexible, and cost-efficient than other existing simulators for large-scale quantum circuit simulations, making it a viable option for researchers with limited access to supercomputers.

Abstract: The relationship between cryptography and learning theory has long been a central theme in the foundations of theoretical computer science: cryptographic primitives can imply hardness of learning, while hardness of learning can in turn be used to construct cryptographic schemes. Recent works have begun exploring analogous connections in the quantum setting, relating the average-case hardness of learning quantum states (AHL) to cryptographic primitives such as one-way state generators (OWSG). Despite recent progress exploring this for pure states, the relationship for mixed states has remained an open question.
  In this work, we prove that the existence of AHL for mixed quantum states is equivalent to the existence of inefficiently verifiable one-way state generators (IV-OWSGs). As a consequence, this relates mixed-state AHL to EFI pairs. Moreover, as a corollary of existing results, we obtain a separation between IV-OWSGs and OWSGs relative to the SWAP oracle.

Abstract: Cavity-mediated collective-spin interactions are commonly described by a quadratic one-axis twisting Hamiltonian. However, the underlying atom-light interaction naturally generates nonlinearities to arbitrary order. Here, we derive a closed-form analytical expression for the complete hierarchy of cavity-mediated collective-spin interactions. We show that the nonlinear coefficients $χ_k$ are governed by Chebyshev polynomials, with $k$ the order of nonlinearity. This yields a universal scaling $χ_k\proptoη^k$ with the single-atom cooperativity $η$ and a description of their dependence on cavity detuning. The result provides a systematic framework for determining when higher-order nonlinearities become relevant and when the quadratic approximation breaks down. We identify experimentally relevant regimes in which higher-order terms substantially modify collective-spin dynamics, accelerating the generation of quantum correlations and quantum Fisher information, and demonstrate that finite-order expansions can accurately reproduce the full cavity-mediated evolution. Our results establish a general framework for understanding higher-order nonlinearities in cavity quantum electrodynamics and their role in collective entanglement and quantum-enhanced sensing.

Abstract: Quantum processors with native multi-qubit gates may offer very efficient implementations of near-term quantum algorithms on noisy hardware. Here, we introduce the multiunimon, a superconducting multimode circuit that encodes multiple qubits and enables native multi-qubit gates in a device consisting of a single Josephson junction embedded in a coplanar waveguide structure. Closely related to the unimon qubit, it inherits properties such as high anharmonicity, full protection against low-frequency charge noise, and partial protection against flux noise. By designing such a three-qubit device with Josephson-to-inductive energy ratio above unity and using a leakage-aware encoding scheme for the computational states, we simulate all twelve different controlled-controlled-NOT gates with a mean fidelity of 99.5% with simple sine-squared pulses of comparable length to single-qubit gates. The performance is limited by incoherent errors dominated by dielectric loss. With improvements in noise protection, design, and pulse shaping, the simulations suggest that fidelities approaching 99.99% are within reach. Our results demonstrate the potential of the multiunimon as a highly connected multi-qubit unit for larger superconducting quantum processors.

Abstract: Quantum functional programming has been developed through two distinct paradigms in the last few years: Quantum Signal Processing (QSP)-based methods, including the Quantum Singular Value Transformation (QSVT), and methods based on higher-order quantum transformations, such as the Universal Hamiltonian Eigenvalue Transformation (UHET). While UHET performs functional transformations of Hamiltonian dynamics, its relationship to QSP-based techniques has remained unclear despite evident structural similarities. In this work, we resolve this gap by establishing a connection between UHET and QSP-based frameworks; specifically, we show that UHET can be interpreted as a (randomised) linearisation of Generalised QSP (GQSP). Building on this result, we introduce a linearised variant of (Hamiltonian-based) QSVT, which we call Universal Hamiltonian Singular Value Transformation (UHSVT), that enables the efficient transformation of the singular values of any arbitrary matrix $A$ encoded in a block of a Hamiltonian, whose dynamics is accessible as a black box, by any sufficiently differentiable complex-valued function $f$. Abstract: The central singularity in gravitational collapse represents a fundamental breakdown of classical general relativity, yet its resolution remains an open challenge. We then feed the most general scalar-vector-tensor theory with second-order equations of motion through this framework. Recasting the field equations in the effective Einstein form $G_{μν}=T^{\rm eff}_{μν}/G_4(Φ,X)$ and evaluating every operator of $L_i$ against the falloff table, we condense the outcome into a constraint table for the coupling functionals and their derivatives at the asymptotic vacuum, sharpened by the scalar and vector equations of motion and vacuum stability. Remarkably few conditions survive. The scalar potential must vanish to cubic order at the asymptotic vacuum, the asymptotic Newton constant must be finite and positive, the scalar and vector modes must be canonically normalized, and the conformally coupled sector carries a frame subtlety. Every other coupling functional is protected by the structure of the theory. The constraint table thus provides a diagnostic for screening beyond-GR models against asymptotic flatness and establishes the BMS group as the asymptotic symmetry group across the entire admissible SVT class.

Because f_R = 1, the metric equations remain second order and the matter problem reduces to a local algebraic reconstruction. For an anisotropic source, we derive unified inversion formulas for (rho, p_r, p_t) in terms of the effective source and the dimensionless trace variable chi = lambda T/(4 pi), and show that the admissible matter branch is the vacuum-connected real root of a cubic equation. The resulting parameter space contains regular positive-density and negative-density throat regimes, a critical boundary where the reconstruction degenerates, and a nonadmissible branch-failure sector. The geometry itself is branch-independent, but the reconstructed matter depends on the matter-Lagrangian prescription. On the admissible branch the radial NEC still fails at the throat, so the model localizes exoticity rather than removing it. The Ricci support and reconstructed matter remain confined to a finite interval, while the exterior tidal field is exactly Schwarzschild with ADM mass M. The results should be read as controlled existence and viability statements within the displayed ansatz families, not as a stability proof.

Method: 利用 A_5 作为 Aut(V_{L_2})≅PSL_2(C) 的二十面体子群作用在格顶点算子代数 V_{L_2} 上，通过研究不动点子代数 V_{L_2}^{A_5} 的结构，借助已知的顶点算子代数有限群作用理论和 C_2-余有限性的判定方法进行证明。

Result: 证明了不动点顶点算子代数 V_{L_2}^{A_5} 是 C_2-余有限的，并且是强有理的顶点算子代数。

Conclusion: 该结果表明二十面体群 A_5 作用下格顶点算子代数的不动点代数具有良好的有限性和理性性质，为格顶点算子代数对称性的研究提供了新的案例。

Abstract: Let $L_2=\mathbb{Z}α$ be the rank-one root lattice with $(α,α)=2$, and let $A_5$ act on the lattice vertex operator algebra $V_{L_2}$ through an icosahedral subgroup of $\operatorname{Aut}(V_{L_2})\cong PSL_2(\mathbb{C})$. We prove that the fixed-point vertex operator algebra $V_{L_2}^{A_5}$ is $C_2$-cofinite and strongly rational.

</details>

### [83] [A refined Schwarz lemma for $V$-harmonic maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13682)
*Guangwen Zhao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了V-调和映射的一个精细Schwarz引理：当定义域为Bakry–Émery Ricci曲率下界为-A的完备黎曼流形，值域为截面曲率上界为-B的黎曼流形，且u具有β阶广义伸缩比时，成立u^*h ≤ (Aβ²/B)g。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Schwarz引理在调和映射及V-调和映射中已有多种推广，本文旨在通过引入"广义伸缩比"（generalized dilatation）的概念，获得一个更精细的Schwarz型估计，从而推广并改进已有的结果。

Method: 利用Bakry–Émery Ricci曲率的下界条件与值域截面曲率的上界条件，结合V-调和映射的方程与广义伸缩比的β阶控制，通过张力场的逐点估计和极值原理推导出拉回度量与定义域度量的不等式关系。

Result: 证明了对于从完备黎曼流形（Bakry–Émery Ricci曲率≥-A）到截面曲率≤-B的黎曼流形的V-调和映射u，若其广义伸缩比为β阶，则成立u^*h ≤ (Aβ²/B)g。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了V-调和映射情形下Schwarz引理的精细版本，将伸缩比、曲率界与映射的微分上界之间建立了精确的定量联系。

Abstract: In this note, we proved a refined Schwarz lemmas for $V$-harmonic maps. Specifically, we prove that if $u$ is a $V$-harmonic map of generalized dilatation of order $β$ from a complete Riemannian manifold with Bakry--Émery Ricci curvature bounded below by a constant $-A$ to a Riemannian manifold with sectional curvature bounded above by a negative constant $-B$, then $u^*h\le \frac{Aβ^2}{B}g$.

</details>


### [84] [Normal curvature of immersed tori of dimension at most $18$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13726)
*Matteo Raffaelli*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了当n维环面T^n光滑浸入单位球B^q⊂R^q且n≤18时，存在某一点使得|II(u,u)|²的球面平均至少为3n/(n+2)，从而在相应维度上回答了Petrunin提出的问题。证明结合了环面的数量曲率障碍与共形拉普拉斯算子论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Petrunin提出了一个关于环面浸入单位球时第二基本形式（第二基本型）在某点球面平均下界的问题。该论文旨在证明在低维（n≤18）情形下这一下界成立，即存在一点使得|II(u,u)|²的球面平均值至少达到3n/(n+2)。

Method: 证明的核心方法是将两个关键工具结合起来：(1) 数量曲率障碍——利用环面T^n不允许正数量曲率度量这一已知几何事实（与正质量定理或标量曲率刚性相关）；(2) 共形拉普拉斯算子论证——通过共形变换将浸入诱导的度量变换为适当的共形度量，并利用共形拉普拉斯算子的谱性质或特征值估计来获得所需的下界。

Result: 该论文成功证明了在n≤18维的情形下，n维环面T^n光滑浸入单位球B^q⊂R^q时，存在某点使得|II(u,u)|²的球面平均至少为3n/(n+2)，从而回答了Petrunin在相应维度上的问题。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过数量曲率障碍与共形拉普拉斯算子方法的巧妙结合，可以在低维（n≤18）情形下证明环面浸入单位球时的第二基本形式球面平均下界，部分解决了Petrunin提出的问题，但高维（n≥19）的情形仍有待进一步研究。

Abstract: We prove that if the $n$-dimensional torus $T^n$ is smoothly immersed in the unit ball $B^q\subset\mathbb R^q$ and $n\leq 18$, then there exists a point at which the spherical average of $\lvert II(u,u)\rvert^2$ is at least $3n/(n+2)$. This answers a question of Petrunin in these dimensions. The proof combines the scalar-curvature obstruction for the torus with a conformal-Laplacian argument.

</details>


### [85] [The Fino-Vezzoni conjecture on balanced Bismut torsion-parallel manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13887)
*Shuwen Chen,Fangyang Zheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Fino-Vezzoni猜想，证明了一个同时具有平衡Bismut挠率平行Hermitian度量和pluriclosed Hermitian度量的紧致复流形必然具有Kähler度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fino-Vezzoni猜想是复几何中的一个重要公开问题，它涉及非Kähler流形上的两类特殊Hermitian度量之间的关系。该猜想断言如果一个紧致复流形同时具有balanced度量和pluriclosed度量，则它一定具有Kähler度量。本文旨在在带有平衡Bismut挠率平行（balanced Bismut torsion-parallel, BBTP）度量的紧致复流形上验证该猜想。

Method: 本文采用几何分析的方法，研究同时具有balanced Bismut torsion-parallel Hermitian度量与pluriclosed Hermitian度量的紧致复流形。通过利用BBTP度量与pluriclosed度量的结构性质，结合紧致复流形上的整体几何工具，证明此类流形必然承认Kähler度量。

Result: 证明了在紧致复流形上，如果它承认一个balanced Bismut torsion-parallel Hermitian度量，并且还承认一个pluriclosed Hermitian度量，则该流形必然具有Kähler度量。这为Fino-Vezzoni猜想在这一类特殊流形上提供了肯定答案。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了一个重要的充分条件：在带有BBTP度量的紧致复流形上，Fino-Vezzoni猜想成立。这推进了对非Kähler流形上特殊Hermitian度量结构及其与Kähler性关系的理解。

Abstract: We study the Fino-Vezzoni conjecture on compact complex manifolds carrying a balanced Bismut torsion-parallel Hermitian metric. We prove that if such a manifold also admits a pluriclosed Hermitian metric, then it admits a Kähler metric.

</details>


### [86] [Stable Free Boundary Minimal Hypersurfaces in $\mathbb{B}^5$ and $\mathbb{B}^6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14037)
*Han Hong,Yujie Wu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在闭欧几里得单位球B^{n+1}中，对于n≤5，不存在完备的双侧稳定浸入自由边界极小超曲面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由边界极小超曲面的存在性与稳定性是几何分析中的核心问题，特别是在闭单位球中建立非存在性结果有助于理解极小曲面理论的边界约束条件。

Method: 通过严格的数学证明（具体证明技术需从全文获取），利用稳定性条件以及维度限制n≤5来推导矛盾。

Result: 证明了在n≤5时，闭欧几里得单位球中不存在完备的双侧稳定浸入自由边界极小超曲面。

Conclusion: 该非存在性结果扩展了对自由边界极小超曲面稳定性的理解，在低维情形下给出了完整的否定性结论。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove that there is no complete two-sided stable immersed free boundary minimal hypersurface in the closed Euclidean unit ball $\mathbb{B}^{n+1}$ for $n\leq 5$.

</details>


### [87] [Second-Order Departure of the Gigli--Mantegazza Flow from Ricci Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14039)
*Dongwoo Gang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了Gigli–Mantegazza构造（通过热核嵌入拉回Wasserstein度量）得到的度量族$\tilde{g}_t$与Ricci流在一阶项上一致，但在二阶项上存在偏差；给出了$\tilde{g}_t$的精确二阶展开式，证明差异点态依赖于全曲率张量的二次项，并证明两者间Gromov–Hausdorff距离为$O(t^2)$且在球面上达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gigli–Mantegazza构造从最优传输几何出发，通过热核嵌入定义了流形上的度量族，其与Ricci流在一阶近似上一致。然而此前对二阶及更高阶的差异尚不清楚。理解这一差异对于将最优输运结构视为Ricci流的替代或逼近至关重要，尤其是在Mabuchi几何和流形上的Wasserstein化研究背景下。作者希望精确定量化两者之间的偏差。

Method: 利用热核嵌入$x\mapsto p_t(x,\cdot)d\operatorname{vol}_g$将Wasserstein度量拉回至流形上得到$\tilde{g}_t$，然后对$\tilde{g}_t$在$t=0$附近做逐项Taylor展开，结合热核的短时渐近展开和Ricci曲率的性质，推导其二阶展开系数，并与Ricci流的二阶展开进行比较，最后通过Gromov–Hausdorff距离估计度量族之间的偏差。

Result: 得到了$\tilde{g}_t$的二阶精确展开：$\tilde{g}_t = g - 2t\operatorname{Ric}_g + t^2\left(-\Delta\operatorname{Ric}_g + 2\operatorname{Ric}_g^2 - \frac{2}{3}\mathcal{Q}_g\right) + O_{C^0}(t^3)$，其中$\mathcal{Q}_g$是曲率张量的二次项。Ricci流中也包含$-\Delta\operatorname{Ric}_g$项，两者差异取决于曲率的二次项；特别地，在 Ricci-平坦但非平坦的度量上，Ricci流静止而$\tilde{g}_t$不静止。Gromov–Hausdorff距离为$O(t^2)$，圆球$S^n$显示出该估计的尖锐性。

Conclusion: Gigli–Mantegazza构造与Ricci流虽然在一阶上吻合，但二阶项存在本质差异，这种差异源于全曲率张量的二次贡献（通过$\mathcal{Q}_g$体现）。该结果揭示了最优传输几何（Wasserstein化）与Ricci流之间的精确关系，同时表明在Ricci-平坦非平坦情形下，这一构造不能直接作为Ricci流的替代。

Abstract: For a closed connected Riemannian manifold $(M,g)$, the Gigli--Mantegazza construction pulls back the quadratic Wasserstein metric under the heat kernel embedding $x\mapsto p_t(x,\cdot)\,d\operatorname{vol}_g$. The resulting family $\widetilde{g}_t$ agrees with Ricci flow to first order in $t$, but in general not to second order. We prove that $$ \widetilde{g}_t =g-2t\operatorname{Ric}_g +t^2\left(-Δ\operatorname{Ric}_g +2\operatorname{Ric}_g^2-\frac{2}{3}\mathcal{Q}_g\right) +O_{C^0}(t^3), $$ where $\mathcal{Q}_g$ is quadratic in the full curvature tensor. The term $-Δ\operatorname{Ric}_g$ also occurs in the second-order expansion of Ricci flow, so the discrepancy depends pointwise and quadratically on the curvature. In particular, at a Ricci-flat metric that is not flat, Ricci flow is stationary while $\widetilde{g}_t$ is not. The Gromov--Hausdorff distance between the Gigli--Mantegazza and Ricci-flow metrics is $O(t^2)$, and round spheres show that this estimate is sharp.

</details>


### [88] [Constructions and Rigidity of Nondegenerate $\mathbb{Z}_2$ Harmonic Functions with Quadric Branching Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14040)
*Yuanbo Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究欧氏空间中分支轨迹为椭球面和平面二次曲线的非退化Z2调和函数。证明了Yan椭球族的参数映射是单射，结合满射性结果将椭球模型与指数为n-1、临界值为正的调和二次多项式在欧氏运动和整体符号意义下等同起来。在R3中，利用修正椭球坐标在任意平面双曲线邻域构造非退化Z2调和函数，并证明以平面双曲线为分支轨迹的全局临界Z2调和函数在无穷远处不可能有有限Almgren频率。利用修正抛物坐标构造以任意指定平面抛物线为分支轨迹的全局非退化Z2调和函数，得到精确渐近展开和尺度缩小极限，并将抛物模型识别为Joyce构造的特殊Lagrangian三维簇族的无穷小双值图势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在系统理解Z2调和函数的分类问题，特别是分支轨迹为二次曲面（椭球面、平面二次曲线）的情形，并建立这些调和模型与特殊几何结构（如特殊Lagrangian簇）之间的联系。

Method: 采用参数映射单射性证明、修正椭球坐标和修正抛物坐标等坐标变换方法，结合Almgren频率分析进行全局正则性研究，并通过渐近展开与尺度缩小极限识别几何模型。

Result: 1) 证明Yan椭球族参数映射为单射，结合已有满射性完成椭球模型的分类识别；2) 在R3中构造以平面双曲线为分支轨迹的局部非退化Z2调和函数，并证明此类全局临界函数不存在（无穷远处Almgren频率发散）；3) 构造以任意平面抛物线为分支轨迹的全局非退化Z2调和函数，给出精确渐近展开，并将其识别为Joyce特殊Lagrangian三维簇的无穷小双值图势。

Conclusion: 本文对Z2调和函数的二次分支轨迹情形给出了完整的分类刻画：椭球模型与二次谐波多项式等价，双曲线情形仅有局部存在性而无全局有限频率解，抛物线情形具有全局解且对应特定的特殊Lagrangian几何结构。

Abstract: We study nondegenerate $\mathbb Z_2$ harmonic functions on Euclidean spaces with branching loci given by ellipsoids and planar conics. We first prove that the parameter map associated with Yan's ellipsoidal family is injective. Together with Yan's surjectivity result, this identifies the ellipsoidal models, up to Euclidean motions and overall sign, with nondegenerate harmonic quadratic polynomials of index \(n-1\) and positive critical value.
  In \(\mathbb R^3\), we use modified ellipsoidal coordinates to construct a nondegenerate $\mathbb Z_2$ harmonic function in a neighborhood of any planar hyperbola. We then show that no global critical $\mathbb Z_2$ harmonic function with a planar hyperbola as branch locus can have finite Almgren frequency at infinity. Using modified paraboloidal coordinates, we construct a global nondegenerate $\mathbb Z_2$ harmonic function with any prescribed planar parabola as branch locus and obtain a precise asymptotic expansion and scale-down limit. Finally, we identify the parabolic model as the infinitesimal two-valued graph potential of a family of special Lagrangian three-folds constructed by Joyce.

</details>


### [89] [Fundamental group of compact decent spacetimes with lightlike hypersurface curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14187)
*Raymond Hounnonkpe,Siba Kalivogui*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究具有类光超曲面曲率的紧致4维时空的基本群，证明了当叶状结构的叶子紧致时，时空的基本群是多循环群；同时证明了非完全病态的4维紧致pp波时空也具有多循环基本群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解紧致时空的拓扑结构，特别是通过基本群来刻画其拓扑性质。几何流形中的类光（null）结构在相对论和全局几何中具有重要地位，而研究这些流形的基本群能够揭示其拓扑约束和代数结构。

Method: 利用类光超曲面曲率诱导的完全测地零超曲面叶状结构，结合紧致叶子假设，通过分析叶状结构的拓扑性质与基本群之间的关系，证明基本群的多循环性。对于pp波时空，利用其非完全病态（non-totally vicious）条件推导基本群的代数性质。

Result: 证明了：(1) 当完全测地零超曲面叶状结构的叶子紧致时，紧致4维时空的基本群是多循环群；(2) 非完全病态的4维紧致pp波时空的基本群也是多循环群。

Conclusion: 类光几何结构（类光超曲面曲率和pp波时空）对紧致4维时空的基本群施加了严格的代数限制，即多循环性，这为理解此类时空的拓扑结构提供了重要刻画。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the fundamental group of compact de cent 4-dimensional spacetimes with lightlike hypersurface curvature. Such manifolds are foliated by totally geodesic null hypersurfaces. We prove that when the leaves of the foliation are compact then the fun damental group of the spacetime is polycyclic. We also prove that a non totally vicious 4-dimensional compact pp-wave spacetime has polycyclic fundamental group.

</details>


### [90] [Ollivier--Ricci Curvature on Groups of Polynomial Growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14232)
*Camillo Brena,Elia Bruè*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究多项式增长群的Cayley图上的Ollivier-Ricci曲率，证明非负Ollivier-Ricci曲率迫使群是虚拟阿贝尔群，并由此推出多项式增长且具有非负Ollivier-Ricci曲率的连通顶点传递图拟等距于Z^k。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Ollivier-Ricci曲率这一离散几何工具对群结构及图的几何性质的反向约束，建立曲率条件与群代数结构之间的联系。

Method: 利用Ollivier-Ricci曲率在Cayley图上的定义，结合多项式增长群的几何与代数的已知性质（如Bass-Guivarc'h公式、Gromov多项式增长定理等），通过曲率非负性推导群的虚拟阿贝尔性，再借助顶点传递图与Cayley图的关系得到图的分类结果。

Result: 证明多项式增长群的Cayley图具有非负Ollivier-Ricci曲率当且仅当该群为虚拟阿贝尔群；作为推论，多项式增长且具有非负Ollivier-Ricci曲率的连通顶点传递图拟等距于Z^k。

Conclusion: Ollivier-Ricci曲率的非负性在多项式增长的背景下等价于群的虚拟阿贝尔性，这一结果为离散曲率与群论之间提供了新的桥梁，并给出了顶点传递图的一个刚性分类结果。

Abstract: We study Ollivier--Ricci curvature on Cayley graphs of groups of polynomial growth. Our main result shows that non-negative Ollivier--Ricci curvature forces the group to be virtually abelian. As an application, we prove that connected vertex-transitive graphs of polynomial growth and non-negative Ollivier--Ricci curvature are quasi-isometric to $\mathbb{Z}^k$, for some $k\in\mathbb{N}$.

</details>


### [91] [One-sided type-D Ricci-flat multi-centre metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14410)
*Yu Chen*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文利用Tod's ansatz的简化形式（基于LeBrun-Tod ansatz）构造单边态D型Ricci平坦多中心度量，这类度量是Hermitian非Kähler且共形Kähler的，由一对生成势决定（3D平直空间中的轴对称调和函数及其调和共轭）。作者系统研究了这些度量的rod结构、渐近结构，并恢复了多个已知闭式例子，还证明了n≤3时这些度量在逆散射框架下自由孤子数比幻影孤子数多2或3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造并系统研究一类新的Ricci平坦多中心度量，这些度量满足Hermitian非Kähler性质且共形Kähler，通过简化Tod's ansatz来获得更多显式可解的度量子类，并与逆散射方法构造的多孤子解建立联系。

Method: 采用Tod's ansatz的简化形式（基于LeBrun-Tod ansatz），利用一对生成势——3D辅助平直空间中的轴对称调和函数（与其原始提议不同）及其调和共轭——来构造单边态D型Ricci平坦多中心度量。系统分析其rod结构、渐近结构，并与逆散射方法的多孤子解框架进行对比。

Result: 构造了单边态D型Ricci平坦多中心度量族；这些度量Hermitian非Kähler且共形Kähler；恢复了多个已知闭式例子；证明了n≤3时这些度量在自由孤子数与幻影孤子数之差为2或3，并猜想对一般n成立。

Conclusion: 通过简化Tod's ansatz成功构建了一类新的Ricci平坦多中心指标族，并建立了与逆散射多孤子解之间的联系，提出了关于自由/幻影孤子数之差的猜想。

Abstract: We employ a simplified form of Tod's ansatz---which is built on the LeBrun--Tod ansatz---to construct one-sided type-D Ricci-flat multi-centre metrics. These metrics are Hermitian non-K{ä}hler and conformally K{ä}hler, and they are determined by a pair of generating potentials: an axisymmetric harmonic function (different from the one originally proposed by Tod) in an auxiliary 3D flat space and its harmonic conjugate. We carry out a systematic study of these multi-centre metrics, including their rod structure, asymptotic structure and recovering from them various known closed-form examples. In particular, we show that, when fitted into the scheme of multi-soliton solutions on flat space constructed by the present author using the inverse-scattering method, these metrics, with number of centres $n\le 3$, have a free-soliton number 2 or 3 greater than their phantom-soliton number; we conjecture that this is true for general $n$.

</details>

Abstract: We compute Simons type equations for parallel mean curvature submanifolds in complex space forms N^n(c), with constant holomorphic sectional curvature c, and product spaces N^n(c)xR. These formulas are then used to characterize some of these submanifolds.

Result: 基依赖的Lyra尺度保持普通可分离性障碍，而纤维依赖的尺度允许加权补偿律从而产生真正不可分离的扭曲函数；混合方程揭示了新颖的刚性现象；对多类势场获得了Einstein约化；给出平坦与非平坦精确例子以及局部和紧致不存在性结果。

Conclusion: Lyra尺度与扭曲函数的相互作用在决定孤立子可分离性方面具有决定性意义，纤维依赖尺度的加权补偿律为构造非平凡扭曲孤立子提供了新途径，同时Einstein约化和不存在性结果展示了该几何框架的丰富结构。

Abstract: This paper defines Lyra almost Ricci-Bourguignon solitons on twisted warped product manifolds and investigates the interaction between the twisting function, the Lyra scale, and the soliton potential field. We derive the horizontal, vertical, and mixed components of the soliton equation and obtain trace, gradient, and factor-inheritance characterizations. The mixed equation yields new rigidity phenomena: a base-dependent Lyra scale preserves the ordinary separability obstruction, whereas a fiber-dependent scale admits a weighted compensation law that allows genuinely nonseparable twisting functions. We also establish Einstein reductions for conformal, Killing, homothetic, concurrent, and gradient potential fields. Exact flat and non-flat examples, together with local and compact nonexistence results, demonstrate the geometric significance of the scale?twisting interaction.

</details>


### [94] [A kinematic explanation of the 1:3 ratio for rolling spheres and the exceptional simple Lie group of rank two](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14442)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有多个证明表明仅在半径比为1:3时，滚动球分布的李代数同构于秩2例外单李代数（分裂实形式），但这些证明缺乏一个直观的运动学解释来阐明为何这个看似神奇的例外单李群会出现在此情境中。作者旨在填补这一概念空白。

Method: 作者不依赖分裂八元数框架，而是通过Tanaka延拓直接构造例外单李群，利用该构造来可视化底层几何。其核心方法是将两个半径比1:3的球体滚动问题与一对单参数子群的交点相联系，由此给出半径比为何必须为3的运动学解释。

Result: 作者成功提供了该例外对称性出现的直接运动学解释：半径比3:1对应于一对单参数子群的特定交点条件，这一条件在该比例下自然涌现。通过Tanaka延拓直接构造例外单李群，避免了分裂八元数的使用，从而更直观地揭示了底层几何结构。

Conclusion: 该论文为1:3半径比下例外单李代数出现的现象提供了期待已久的运动学解释，并通过Tanaka延拓实现了无需分裂八元数的直接构造，为理解和可视化这一经典结果提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Given a pair of (round) 2-dimensional spheres, one of which has radius three times that of the other, the Lie algebra of local infinitesimal symmetries for the distribution corresponding to rolling the spheres along each other (without letting them slip or twist) happens to be isomorphic to the split real form of the exceptional simple Lie algebra of rank 2. These exceptional local symmetries appear only for this specific 1:3 ratio of radii, however, and while there are several proofs of this result, a straightforward kinematic explanation for the seemingly miraculous appearance of an exceptional simple Lie group in this situation has long been desired. In this paper, we provide such an explanation, relating the ratio of radii to the intersections of a pair of one-parameter subgroups that can be seen from the rolling sphere perspective. The approach does not require the split-octonions, as we construct the exceptional simple Lie group directly by Tanaka prolongation to aid in visualizing the underlying geometry.

</details>


### [95] [Hermitian curvature flow and HKT geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14358)
*Beatrice Brienza,Jeffrey Streets*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个保持HKT几何的Hermitian曲率流，其不动点即为HKT-Einstein度量，并验证了Verbitsky在四元数Monge-Ampère方程背景下提出的流。论文发现了正则性障碍和Chern标量曲率的单调性公式，提出了最大存在时间猜想并给出条件性解决，在四维情形证明了存在性猜想。此外，在四元数Hopf曲面上构造了第一个非齐次的HKT-Einstein度量发散序列，并分类了由李群双不变度量诱导的强HKT结构中哪些也是HKT-Einstein的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HKT（超Kähler with Torsion）几何在超弦理论、广义复几何和四元数几何中具有重要地位。HKT-Einstein度量是四元数Monge-Ampère方程的解，其存在性与收敛性长期缺乏系统研究。Verbitsky此前提出过一个相关曲率流，但缺乏严格分析。本文旨在为该流建立严格的解析框架，探索其正则性、单调性结构与收敛性，并首次构造非齐次例子以揭示收敛问题的微妙性。

Method: 本文定义了一个保持HKT几何结构的Hermitian曲率流（HKT流），该流的不动点恰好是HKT-Einstein度量。作者通过分析Chern标量曲率沿流的演化，建立了单调性公式，并揭示了基本正则性障碍。在四维情形，利用特定的几何解析技术证明了流的长时存在性。此外，通过对quaternionic Hopf曲面上度量的显式构造，给出了非齐次HKT-Einstein度量的发散序列；最后对李群上的双不变强HKT结构进行了完整的HKT-Einstein分类。

Result: 主要结果包括：（1）构造了保持HKT几何的曲率流并建立其与Verbitsky流的关系；（2）发现Chern标量曲率的单调性公式及正则性障碍；（3）提出最大存在时间猜想并给出条件性解决，在四维情形完整证明了存在性；（4）首次在四元数Hopf曲面上构造非齐次HKT-Einstein度量的发散序列，表明收敛的微妙性；（5）完整分类了李群双不变度量下的强HKT结构中HKT-Einstein的情形。

Conclusion: 该工作为HKT几何中的曲率流理论奠定了坚实基础，确认了HKT流与四元数Monge-Ampère方程的深刻联系。非齐次发散例子的存在说明HKT-Einstein度量的收敛问题远比齐次情形复杂，这为后续研究指明了重要方向。李群上的分类结果则为构造更多显式例子提供了代数框架。

Abstract: We identify a Hermitian curvature flow which preserves HKT geometry, and whose fixed points are HKT-Einstein metrics, equivalent to a flow suggested by Verbitsky in the context of the quaternionic Monge-Ampère equation. We exhibit a fundamental regularity obstruction and a monotonicity formula for the Chern scalar curvature. We formulate a maximal existence time conjecture for this flow, and give a conditional resolution. We establish the existence conjecture in dimension four. We show the existence of a divergent sequence of HKT-Einstein metrics on quaternionic Hopf surfaces. These are the first non-homogeneous examples in the literature, and indicate the delicacy of the convergence question. Finally we classify which strong HKT structures arising from bi-invariant metrics on Lie groups are also HKT-Einstein.

</details>


### [96] [Classification of compact Lagrangian self-similar submanifolds with Legendrian capillary boundary in the unit ball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14431)
*Dong Gao,Yong Luo,Hui Ma,Jiabin Yin*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文完整分类了闭单位球内满足 H+εX⊥=0（ε∈{-1,0,1}）的光滑紧致连通Lagrangian浸入子流形X，其边界为球面上的Legendre边界且各连通分支具有常数接触角。证明边界至多有两个连通分支；边界连通时X微分同胚于赤道Lagrangian n-盘；边界有两个分支时X全局裂变为γ(s)ψ(p)的形式，其中ψ为单位球面中的紧致极小腿浸入，γ为具有唯一径向最小值的Anciaux剖面，两个接触角互补。在复二维情形下，所有非盘解均为Lagrangian catenoid段（ε=0）或旋转Anciaux环面（ε=±1）的有限覆盖；在高维情形下，迭代Calabi提升构造出具有非平凡拓扑极小Legendre链的族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对Lagrangian子流形在球体边界条件下满足H+εX⊥=0这一几何方程（涵盖极小、常平均曲率和Lagrangian自相似解）进行完整分类，深入理解Lagrangian catenoid和Anciaux环面的结构，并推广到高维情形。

Method: 采用几何分析中关于Lagrangian子流形接触角和边界结构的深度几何论证，结合全局分裂定理、Anciaux剖面的径向分析、Calabi提升构造以及复二维情形的有限覆盖分类技术。

Result: 边界至多有两个连通分支；单连通边界对应赤道Lagrangian盘；双连通边界全局分裂为剖面乘以极小腿浸入形式，两接触角互补。复二维中所有非盘解均为Lagrangian catenoid段(ε=0)或旋转Anciaux环面(ε=±1)的有限覆盖；高维中通过迭代Calabi提升构造出非平凡拓扑的极小腿链族。

Conclusion: 该分类定理完整刻画了球体内满足H+εX⊥=0的Lagrangian浸入子流形结构，揭示了低维与高维情形之间的本质差异，并建立了与极小腿嵌入分类的深层联系。

Abstract: We classify smooth compact connected Lagrangian immersions $X$ in the closed unit ball of $\C^n$, $n\ge2$, satisfying $H+\varepsilon X^\perp=0$, $\varepsilon\in\{-1,0,1\}$, with Legendrian boundary on the unit sphere and constant contact angle on each connected component. We prove that the boundary has at most two connected components. When the boundary is connected, $X$ is a diffeomorphism onto an equatorial Lagrangian $n$-disk. When the boundary has two components, $X$ splits globally as $X(s,p)=γ(s)ψ(p)$, where $ψ$ is a compact minimal Legendrian immersion in the unit sphere and $γ$ is an Anciaux profile with a unique radial minimum. The two contact angles are supplementary. In complex dimension two, every non-disk solution is a finite cover of a Lagrangian catenoid segment for $\varepsilon=0$ or of a rotational Anciaux annulus for $\varepsilon=\pm1$. In higher complex dimensions, iterated Calabi suspensions produce families whose minimal Legendrian links have nontrivial topology.

</details>


### [97] [Positive Scalar Curvature and Volume Growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14438)
*Bochao Kong,Xingyu Zhu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文在非负Ricci曲率的完备黎曼流形上证明了两个尖锐的体积增长阶估计，从而解决了Gromov于1986年提出的猜想：单位球体积的均匀亏量（正宏观数量曲率的类比）迫使余维一的体积增长；均匀正标量曲率下界迫使余维二的体积增长（即余维二体积增长猜想）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gromov在1986年提出了关于非负Ricci曲率流形上体积增长阶数的猜想，这些猜想将曲率的正性条件与流形的拓扑/几何增长行为联系起来。解决这些猜想有助于理解正曲率流形的结构限制，是几何分析领域长期悬而未决的重要问题。

Method: 论文采用几何分析的方法，通过研究单位球体积的亏量（deficit）与流形整体体积增长阶数之间的联系，利用比较几何、Ricci曲率条件下的体积估计技术，证明了两个体积增长阶的尖锐（sharp）估计。

Result: 证明了两个结果：(1) 单位球体积的均匀亏量（正宏观标量曲率的类比条件）迫使流形具有余维一（codimension one）的体积增长；(2) 均匀正标量曲率下界迫使流形具有余维二（codimension two）的体积增长，即解决了Gromov的余维二体积增长猜想。两个估计均为尖锐的。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了Gromov于1986年提出的两个体积增长阶猜想，为非负Ricci曲率流形上正标量曲率条件与体积增长行为之间的关系提供了精确的刻画，前者对应余维一增长，后者对应余维二增长。

Abstract: For a complete Riemannian manifold with nonnegative Ricci curvature, we prove two sharp volume growth order estimates, thereby resolve a conjecture of Gromov in 1986. There first is that a uniform deficit in the volume of unit balls, an analog of positive macroscopic scalar curvature, forces codimension one volume growth, and the second one is that a uniformly positive scalar curvature lower bound forces codimension two growth known as the codimension two volume growth conjecture.

</details>


### [98] [Almost isoclinic Lagrangian submanifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14444)
*Tang-Kai Lee,Mu-Tao Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在定向Lagrangian Grassmannian $\mathrm{Lag}^+(n)$中引入"几乎等斜区域"(almost isoclinic region)，这是$\mathrm{Lag}^+(2)\simeq \mathbb{S}^1\times\mathbb{S}^2$中自然凸区域的高维推广。在该区域上构造典范正函数$\Lambda$，证明$\log\Lambda$关于不变Grassmannian度量是凹的，由此得到极小子流形和Lagrangian平均曲率流的次调和性与单调性公式，并应用于刚性与Bernstein型定理的证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的等斜条件(characteristic angles均匀接近)在二维情形对应一个自然的凸区域，但缺乏内在的高维推广。作者希望建立高维情形下类似的几何框架，使得极小Lagrangian子流形和Lagrangian平均曲率流能够获得单调性、刚性及Bernstein型结论。

Method: 1) 在定向Lagrangian Grassmannian中定义几乎等斜区域，作为二维情形的推广。2) 在此区域上构造典范正函数$\Lambda$，并证明$\log\Lambda$在不变Grassmannian度量下是凹的。3) 利用凹性推导Lagrangian子流形Gauss映射的次调和性及LMC流动下的单调性公式。4) 结合渐近锥条件和$\Lambda$的正下界，证明刚性定理。

Result: 证明了$\log\Lambda$的凹性，由此得到最小Lagrangian子流形与Lagrangian平均曲率流的次调和性和单调性公式；作为应用，证明了一个几乎等斜、单端渐近锥形且$\Lambda$有正下界的最小Lagrangian必须是Lagrangian $n$-平面(Bernstein型刚性结论)。

Conclusion: 几乎等斜区域为高维Lagrangian几何中极小性与流的刚性分析提供了一个自然且有效的框架，其核心的凹性性质与单调性公式是后续几何应用的关键工具。

Abstract: We introduce the almost isoclinic region in the oriented Lagrangian Grassmannian ${\rm Lag}^+(n)$ of $\mathbb{C}^n$, an intrinsic higher-dimensional analog of a natural convex region in ${\rm Lag}^+(2) \simeq \mathbb S^1\times \mathbb S^2$. A Lagrangian submanifold is called almost isoclinic if its Gauss map takes values in this region, extending the graphical condition that the characteristic angles of the tangent plane remain uniformly close. We construct a canonical positive function $Λ$ on this region and prove that $\log Λ$ is concave with respect to the invariant Grassmannian metric. This property yields subharmonicity and monotonicity formulas for minimal Lagrangians and Lagrangian mean curvature flow. As applications, we prove rigidity and Bernstein-type results, including that an almost isoclinic asymptotically conical minimal Lagrangian with one end and a positive lower bound for $Λ$ must be a Lagrangian $n$-plane.

</details>


### [99] [The Fisher Metric of the Ricci Flow Heat Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14478)
*Bennett Chow,Robert Koirala*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Ricci流共轭热核相关的Fisher信息度量g^F_τ，证明其具有单调性且不大于背景度量g_t，并通过Fisher缺陷的矩阵平方恒等式给出刚性定理。还发展了该观点的若干推论，包括反向Poincaré不等式、φ-散度的收缩公式、热核分裂映射的构造，以及Fisher度规准则在Bamler ε-正则性定理中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Ricci流研究中，热核度量的几何信息对理解流的奇异性和分裂行为至关重要。作者引入Fisher信息度量来量化基点的移动如何改变点化热核测度，期望获得Ricci流几何的新的单调量和刚性刻画。

Method: 定义与共轭热核相关的Fisher信息度量g^F_τ，并利用Perelman型单调量技术与共轭热核的分析性质证明其单调性。通过建立Fisher缺陷g_t-g^F_τ的矩阵平方恒等式来研究等号情形，并推广到divergence收缩与分裂映射等衍生结果。

Result: 证明了g^F_τ在尺度上单调且g^F_τ≤g_t；在闭流形上恒有严格不等式0<g^F_τ<g_t，而在完备情形等号迫使欧氏分裂。此外获得反向Poincaré不等式、φ-散度收缩公式、热核分裂映射构造，以及Nash熵与Fisher缺陷的相关性和Bamler ε-正则性定理的余维一Fisher准则。

Conclusion: Fisher信息度量提供了研究Ricci流中热核测度几何的新框架，其缺陷恒等式给出分裂的刚性刻画，并展现出在正则性分析中的实际应用价值。

Abstract: We introduce and study a Fisher information metric \(g^F_τ\) associated to the conjugate heat kernel of a Ricci flow \((M^n,g_t)\). This tensor measures, at a fixed scale, how the pointed heat-kernel measure changes when the base point is moved. We prove that \(g^F_τ\) is monotone in scale and satisfies \(g^F_τ\le g_t\). We relate its trace to the pointed Nash entropy and prove a matrix square identity for the Fisher defect \(g_t-g^F_τ.\) This identity gives a rigidity theorem for the equality case; on closed connected flows one has the strict inequalities \(0<g^F_τ<g_t\) at every positive scale, while in the complete case equality forces a Euclidean splitting.
  We also develop several consequences of this point of view. These include a sharp reverse Poincaré inequality for the heat semigroup, a contraction formula for \(\varphi\)-divergences along conjugate heat flow, and a canonical construction of heat-kernel splitting maps from large eigenvalues of the Fisher endomorphism. As applications, we relate pointed Nash entropy close to \(0\) to small Fisher deficit at comparable scales, and we obtain a codimension-one Fisher-metric criterion for applying Bamler's \(\varepsilon\)-regularity theorem.

</details>


### [100] [Universal Volume Growth Bounds from Positive Intermediate Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14507)
*Gioacchino Antonelli*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在完备黎曼流形上，若 Ricci 曲率非负且 (m+1)-中间曲率至少为1，则任意球的体积以 R^m 为阶进行增长控制，并由此推导出当 m=n-2 时，Ricci 非负加数量曲率至少为1可推出体积增长上界为 R^{n-2}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带有曲率条件（如 Ricci 非负与中间曲率下界）的完备黎曼流形上的体积增长估计，是黎曼几何与几何分析中的核心问题。该工作受到 Brendle–Hirsch–Johne 关于中间曲率研究的影响，旨在建立更广泛的体积增长上界定理。

Method: 作者利用 Ricci 曲率非负的条件以及 (m+1)-中间曲率下界，通过几何分析技巧（如比较几何或测地球体积估计的方法）建立了体积增长的上界不等式，并特别处理了 m=n-2 时的退化情形以得到关于数量曲率的推论。

Result: 证明了当 Ric≥0 且 C_{m+1}≥1 时，对任意 p∈M 和 R>0，有 Vol B_R(p) ≤ C(n,m) R^m。特别地，当 m=n-2 时，由 Ric≥0 和 Scal≥1 可推出 Vol B_R(p) ≤ C(n) R^{n-2}。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了中间曲率条件下完备流形的体积增长刚性估计，推广了已有关于 Ricci 或数量曲率情形的结论，并与 Brendle–Hirsch–Johne 引入的中间曲率概念相衔接，为黎曼流形在混合曲率条件下的结构研究提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Let $n,m$ be integers such that $n\geq 2$ and $0\leq m\leq n-2$. Let $(M^n,g)$ be a complete Riemannian manifold, and let $C_{m+1}$ be the $(m+1)$-intermediate curvature introduced by Brendle--Hirsch--Johne. We prove \[
  \mathrm{Ric}\geq0,\qquad C_{m+1}\geq 1
  \quad\Longrightarrow\quad
  \mathrm{Vol} B_R(p)\leq C(n,m)R^m, \] for every $p\in M$ and every $R>0$. In particular, when $m=n-2$, we deduce that \[
  \mathrm{Ric}\geq0,\qquad \mathrm{Scal}\geq 1
  \quad\Longrightarrow\quad
  \mathrm{Vol} B_R(p)\leq C(n)R^{n-2}, \] for every $p\in M$ and every $R>0$.

</details>
