### [101] [Axion Isocurvature Perturbations Survive the Scaling Evolution of Axion Domain Walls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13650)
*Naoya Kitajima,Junseok Lee,Kai Murai,Fuminobu Takahashi,Wen Yin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了由暴胀扰动产生的轴子畴壁的演化，扩展了先前关于缩放机制下超视界关联保留的研究。在大初始扰动情况下（轴子势能多个极小值已布居），尽管宇称混淆机制贡献的长波长等曲率扰动可能被平均化抑制，但畴壁坍缩产生的轴子提供了额外贡献，在壁进入缩放态后湮灭前可占据主导。偏差真空能在壁湮灭时释放，继承了暴胀扰动的超视界关联并将其传递给轴子能量密度，导致可观的等曲率扰动在壁湮灭后仍然存活。同时还讨论了畴壁坍缩产生的暗物质的通用等曲率约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明轴子畴壁网络进入缩放态后仍保留超视界关联，本工作将分析扩展到大初始涨落情形，探究在此情形下等曲率扰动是否仍能存活，以及畴壁坍缩产生的轴子对暗物质等曲率扰动的贡献与约束。

Method: 扩展了先前对轴子畴壁网络的分析，考虑大初始涨落（多个势能极小值已布居），分别分析传统错位机制贡献（多真空平均导致长波等曲率扰动被抑制）与畴壁坍缩产生轴子的贡献（壁进入缩放态后湮灭前占主导）；分析壁湮灭时偏差真空能释放如何继承暴胀扰动的超视界关联并传递给轴子能量密度。

Result: 发现即使壁湮灭后，可观的等曲率扰动仍然存活；畴壁坍缩产生的暗物质受到通用等曲率约束，在特定参数空间中等曲率信号可被观测。

Conclusion: 轴子畴壁坍缩产生的暗物质可以避免传统错位机制中等曲率扰动被多真空平均抑制的问题，其等曲率扰动可能成为检测此类模型的独特可观测信号，需要满足相应的宇宙学约束。

Abstract: We revisit the evolution of axion domain walls seeded by inflationary fluctuations. In our previous work, we showed that such domain-wall networks retain superhorizon correlations even after entering the scaling regime. We extend our previous analysis to the case with large initial fluctuations, where many minima of the axion potential are already populated when the axion starts to oscillate. Although the conventional misalignment contribution can have suppressed long-wavelength isocurvature perturbations when many vacua are averaged over, axions produced by domain-wall collapse provide an additional contribution that can dominate when the walls enter the scaling regime before annihilation. In particular, the biased vacuum energy released during wall annihilation inherits the superhorizon correlations of the inflationary fluctuations and transfers them to the axion energy density. We find that sizable isocurvature perturbations can therefore survive even after the walls annihilate. We also discuss generic isocurvature constraints on dark matter produced by domain-wall collapse.

</details>


### [102] [Gravitational Waves from Dimension-6 Assisted Peccei - Quinn Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13692)
*Nico Benincasa,Kristjan Müürsepp*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出在最小KSVZ轴子模型中加入一个Peccei-Quinn对称性不变的四维-6算符，证明该算符可触发一阶相变，并研究了其引力波信号和暗物质现象学，最后给出多种紫外完整化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小KSVZ轴子模型是标准QCD轴子框架，但其相变结构和早期宇宙热力学行为较为简单。作者希望通过引入一个模型无关的维-6算符来扩展该框架，从而研究能否触发一阶相变，并探索其可观测的宇宙学后果（引力波和暗物质），同时以紫外完整化来验证该模型无关方法的合理性。

Method: 在Peccei-Quinn有效势中引入一个保持PQ对称性的维-6算符（模型无关扩展），在参数空间中将相变性质与现有观测约束相交叉，计算一阶相变产生的引力波谱并评估当前及未来干涉仪的探测能力，同时分析暗物质产生机制；随后构造多种具体紫外完整化模型来实现该维-6算符，并比较其唯象学差异。

Result: 证明该维-6算符能够触发一阶相变，并在满足现有观测约束的参数空间内找到引力波信号可被当前及未来干涉仪探测到的区域；同时给出了该相变背景下的暗物质现象学特征，并展示了多种紫外完整化方案各自产生不同的唯象学信号。

Conclusion: 模型无关的维-6算符扩展为KSVZ轴子模型提供了丰富的宇宙学现象学，包括可探测的引力波信号和独特的暗物质行为；不同紫外完整化导致的信号差异支持采用模型无关方法进行研究。

Abstract: We consider a simple model-independent extension of the minimal KSVZ axion model, augmenting the Peccei - Quinn effective potential with a dimension-6 operator invariant under the Peccei - Quinn symmetry. We show that this operator can trigger a first-order phase transition and study its cosmological consequences. We map out the parameter space, consistent with present observational constraints, in which the phase transition produces a gravitational wave signal observable at current and future interferometers, and we investigate the resulting dark matter phenomenology. Finally, we present specific ultraviolet completions that generate the dimension-6 operator, each leading to distinct phenomenological signatures, which motivates our model-independent approach.

</details>


### [103] [Beyond $Δm^2$: Absolute Mass Sensitivity in Neutrino Oscillations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13736)
*Gustavo F. S. Alves,André L. C. de Gouvêa,Joshua Kaler,Shirley Weishi Li,Pedro A. N. Machado*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了中微子振荡在次领头阶（NLO）对绝对中微子质量的敏感度。传统观点认为中微子振荡只测量质量平方差，但通过波包处理和产生运动学修正，振荡相位在NLO包含与m_i^4-m_j^4成正比的项，从而可探测绝对质量标度。以反应堆反中微子为例，JUNO实验对数百keV量级的中微子质量敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认知认为中微子振荡仅能测量质量平方差（Δm²），无法确定绝对中微子质量标度。然而这只是领头阶（LO）的结论，在NLO修正下振荡相位可能对绝对质量敏感。作者希望探索这一可能性，为确定中微子绝对质量提供新的、互补的观测途径。

Method: 采用波包（wave-packet）处理方法，并考虑中微子产生过程的运动学约束，推导出中微子振荡相位的次领头阶修正项。修正项与Δm²_ij(m_i²+m_j²)成正比，即对中微子绝对质量标度敏感。然后将此结果应用于反应堆反中微子实验场景，评估JUNO实验的灵敏度。

Result: JUNO实验对中微子绝对质量具有数百keV量级的灵敏度。虽然该灵敏度不及现有的β衰变、电子俘获和宇宙学方法所提供的约束，但中微子振荡提供了一种全新的、互补的探测手段，其敏感的物理量组合（m_i²+m_j²）与其他实验所测组合不同。

Conclusion: 中微子振荡在NLO级别确实可以探测绝对中微子质量标度，尽管灵敏度有限，不作为最有力的约束手段，但它提供了一种独特且互补的探针，能够测量与其他方法不同组合的中微子质量参数，具有重要的物理意义和实验价值。

Abstract: Conventional wisdom says that neutrino oscillations measure only mass-squared differences and not the absolute neutrino mass scale. This is true, however, only at leading order in the expansion parameters $m_i/E$, the ratios of the neutrino masses $m_i$ ($i=1,2,3$) to the neutrino energy $E$. At next-to-leading order, the oscillation phase includes terms proportional to $m_i^4-m_j^4 = Δm^2_{ij}(m_i^2+m_j^2)$, and is therefore sensitive to the absolute mass scale. In this paper, we derive the next-to-leading-order corrections using a wave-packet treatment and taking into account the neutrino-production kinematics. We then apply this result to reactor antineutrinos and find that the JUNO experiment is sensitive to neutrino masses of a few hundred keV. While not competitive with existing bounds from beta decay, electron capture, and cosmology, neutrino oscillations provide a novel, complementary probe of the neutrino mass scale, with sensitivity to a different combination of neutrino masses.

</details>


### [104] [Strong $\mathcal{CP}$ and Quark Mass Hierarchies from Modular Invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13796)
*Xiang-Gan Liu,Michael Ratz,Alexander Stewart*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过将模强CP解扩展到非阿贝尔有限模对称性，揭示了一个此前被忽视的模反常条件。该条件使QCD角在正则性要求下与CP破缺模量无关（θ̄=0），同时当模权为正时强制夸克Yukawa行列式在尖点消失，产生显著的夸克质量层级。此外，模对称性作为R对称性运行，在适度附加假设下使QCD角独立于膨胀子（dilaton），并给出了一个显式模型作为说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强CP问题（为何QCD的θ角极小）和夸克质量层级（为何夸克质量差异巨大）是两个基本粒子物理中的开放问题。本文旨在探索它们是否可能源于相同的紫外（高能）物理结构，通过扩展模对称性的方法来统一解释这两个看似无关的现象。

Method: 作者将模强CP解推广到非阿贝尔有限模对称性框架中，系统分析了有限表示中的模反常条件。利用模空间尖点（cusp）附近的正则性（regularity）约束，推导出QCD角θ̄=0的条件，并研究了正模权下Yukawa行列式在尖点的行为。同时论证模对称性作为R对称性的性质，结合附带假设推导QCD角对膨胀子的独立性。

Result: 研究发现了一个此前被忽视的模反常条件，该条件加上正则性要求可消去QCD角对CP破缺模量的依赖，从而保证θ̄=0。对于正模权，Yukawa行列式在尖点处被迫为零，自然产生夸克间的显著质量层级。模对称性作为R对称性的性质使QCD角在合理假设下独立于膨胀子。文中给出显式模型验证了上述机制。

Conclusion: 强CP问题与夸克质量层级确实可以源于相同的紫外结构——即非阿贝尔有限模对称性。该框架不仅解决了强CP问题，还同时解释了夸克质量层级，表明这两个看似独立的基本问题之间可能存在深层的物理联系。模反常条件作为新的理论约束，为构建更完整的高能模型提供了新方向。

Abstract: The strong $\mathcal{CP}$ problem and quark mass hierarchies can probe the same ultraviolet structure. We show this by extending modular strong-$\mathcal{CP}$ solutions to non-Abelian finite modular symmetries. This reveals a previously overlooked modular-anomaly condition from the finite representations. Regularity removes the dependence of the QCD angle on the $\mathcal{CP}$-breaking modulus, yielding $\barθ=0$. For positive modular weight, it also forces the quark Yukawa determinant to vanish at the cusp. This leads to pronounced mass hierarchies among the quarks. We further stress that modular symmetries act as R-symmetries. Under moderate additional assumptions, this renders the QCD angle independent of the dilaton. An explicit model illustrates the mechanism.

</details>


### [105] [Systematic study of fully heavy-flavored tetraquarks $Q_1Q_2\bar{Q}_1\bar{Q}_2\,(Q_{1;2} \in \left\{b;c\right\})$: Mass spectra, threshold analysis, and confrontation with LHC data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14154)
*André Aimé Atangana Likéné,Alexis Franck Rothen,Dieudonné Nga Ongodo,Germain Hubert Ben-Bolie,Tobias Golling*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文采用非相对论性双夸克-反双夸克模型，结合修正的Cornell势和数值求解薛定谔方程，系统计算了全重四夸克态（基态1S及激发态1P、2S、1D、2P、3S、4S）的质谱，预测结果与LHC实验观测到的X(6200)、X(6900)、X(7300)等窄结构相符，为这些结构的分类和实验识别提供了理论支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC上LHCb、CMS和ATLAS合作组正在积极搜寻全重四夸克态，特别是在双J/ψ质量谱中观测到的X(6200)、X(6900)和X(7300)等窄结构引发了广泛的理论兴趣，需要理论模型对这些实验观测进行解释和预测。

Method: 采用非相对论性双夸克-反双夸克模型，将四夸克态建模为轴向矢量双夸克和反双夸克在3̄和3色表示中形成的色单态束缚态。使用修正的Cornell势和三点中心差分方法数值求解薛定谔方程，自旋相关相互作用采用非微扰方式处理。系统研究了S波、P波和D波态的各种J^PC量子数。

Result: 计算得到的质谱与近期LHC观测结果及其他理论计算进行了比较，检验了各态相对于强衰变（fall-apart）的稳定性。结果表明多个实验观测到的结构可被解释为全粲四夸克态的不同激发模式。

Conclusion: 本文结果支持将若干实验观测到的结构解释为全粲四夸克态的不同激发，并为其实验识别提供了有价值的预测。

Abstract: Experimental searches for fully heavy tetraquark states are actively pursued at the LHC. The LHCb, CMS, and ATLAS collaborations have investigated fully charmed $cc\bar{c}\bar{c}$, mixed $bc\bar{b}\bar{c}$, and fully bottom $bb\bar{b}\bar{b}$ tetraquarks. In particular, narrow structures such as $X(6200)$, $X(6900)$, and $X(7300)$ observed in the di-$J/ψ$ mass spectrum have stimulated considerable theoretical interest. We employ a nonrelativistic diquark-antidiquark model to investigate the mass spectra of ground ($1S$) and excited ($1P$, $2S$, $1D$, $2P$, $3S$, and $4S$) fully heavy tetraquark states. The tetraquarks are modeled as color-singlet bound states of axial-vector diquarks and antidiquarks in the $\mathbf{\bar{3}}$ and $\mathbf{3}$ color representations. The Schrödinger equation is solved numerically using a modified Cornell potential and the three-point central difference method, while spin-dependent interactions are treated nonperturbatively. We investigate a broad range of $J^{PC}$ quantum numbers for $S$-, $P$-, and $D$-wave states. The predicted masses are compared with recent LHC observations and other theoretical calculations, and the stability of the states against strong fall-apart decays is examined. Our results support the interpretation of several observed structures as different excitations of fully charmed tetraquarks and provide useful predictions for their experimental identification.

</details>


### [106] [Exploring the chiral magnetic effect in Au+Au collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}}=7.7-200$ GeV through Chiral Anomaly Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14188)
*Zilin Yuan,Anping Huang,Guo-Liang Ma,Mei Huang,Guannan Xie*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该研究利用带手征反常输运(CAT)模块的多相输运(AMPT)模型，研究了Au+Au碰撞在$\sqrt{s_{NN}}=7.7-200$ GeV能量范围内的手征磁效应(CME)。研究发现CME信号在11.5-27 GeV能量范围和20-50%中心度区间内显著存在，且模型与STAR实验数据吻合良好；但在7.7 GeV和200 GeV下CME信号消失，表明CME的可观测性关键取决于磁场动态演化和部分子相化学冻结时间的碰撞能量依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能重离子碰撞中可能产生手征不平衡的夸克局域域，反映量子色动力学(QCD)的拓扑性质，从而诱发夸克-胶子等离子体中的局域P和CP破缺。手征磁效应(CME)预测沿碰撞产生的强磁场方向出现电荷分离，需要通过电荷依赖的方位角关联(Δγ)来研究。作者旨在系统地探究不同碰撞能量下CME信号的强度和可观测性，并检验不同提取方法的一致性。

Method: 采用配备手征反常输运(CAT)模块的多相输运(AMPT)模型，在$\sqrt{s_{NN}}=7.7-200$ GeV范围内模拟Au+Au碰撞。使用两种独立方法提取CME信号：(1)直接相减零手征化学势($μ_5=0$)与有限$μ_5$模拟之间的$\langle N_{part}Δγ\rangle$关联子；(2)事件形状选择(ESS)方法。通过比较两种方法的结果以交叉验证。

Result: 在11.5-27 GeV能量范围和20-50%中心度区间内观察到显著的CME信号，AMPT模型结果与STAR实验数据一致。两种方法提取的CME分数在此能量范围内在不确定性内相互一致。然而，在7.7 GeV和200 GeV能量下CME信号消失。

Conclusion: CME的可观测性强烈依赖于磁场随时间演化的动力学特征以及部分子相的化学冻结时间，这两者均随碰撞能量显著变化。该研究为理解碰撞能量依赖的CME信号提供了系统框架，并验证了不同提取方法在中间能量区间的一致性。

Abstract: High-energy heavy-ion collisions have the potential to create local domains of chirality-imbalanced quarks, reflecting the topological characteristics of quantum chromodynamics. This phenomenon can potentially induce local $\mathcal{P}$ and $\mathcal{CP}$ violations in the quark-gluon plasma. The Chiral Magnetic Effect (CME) predicts an electric charge separation along the intense magnetic field generated during these collisions, which is typically investigated through charge-dependent azimuthal correlations ($Δγ$). In this work, we investigate the CME in Au+Au collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}} = 7.7 - 200$ GeV using a multiphase transport (AMPT) model equipped with a Chiral Anomaly Transport (CAT) module. we employ two independent methods: direct subtraction of the correlator $\langle N_{part}Δγ\rangle$ between simulations with zero and finite chiral chemical potential $μ_5$, and the event-shape-selection (ESS) approach. Our results reveal a significant CME signal within the energy range of 11.5-27 GeV and the centrality range of $20-50\%$, where the AMPT model aligns well with STAR experimental data. Furthermore, the CME fractions extracted by both methods are consistent within uncertainties across these energies. However, the CME signal disappears at both 7.7 and 200 GeV. These findings underscore that the observability of the CME critically depends on both the dynamic evolution of the magnetic field and the chemical freeze-out time of the partonic phase, which vary significantly with collision energy.

</details>


### [107] [Probing the Single Production of First-Generation Singlet Vector-like Leptons at Future $e^+e^-$ Colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14195)
*Yao-Bei Liu,Stefano Moretti*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了未来正负电子对撞机上第一代弱同位旋单态矢量轻子（VLLs）的单产生过程，通过e⁺e⁻→e±E∓通道（E±→W±ν_e和E±→e±Z），利用高度增强的W/Z玻色子衰变产物融合为单个fat-jet的特征进行信号识别。在ILC（√s=1 TeV）和CLIC（√s=1.5 TeV）上进行了全面蒙特卡罗模拟，确定2σ排除与5σ发现能力。结果显示ILC可探测至900 GeV，CLIC可延伸至1400 GeV，超越强子对撞机现有极限并补充电弱精密测量约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索未来正负电子对撞机对第一代弱同位旋单态矢量轻子（VLLs）的探测潜力，因为这类新粒子可能存在于超出标准模型的理论中，而现有强子对撞机的约束有限，需要更灵敏的探测手段来验证或排除该物理场景。

Method: 研究通过单产生通道e⁺e⁻→e±E∓，其中E±分别衰变至W±ν_e和e±Z。利用高能情况下W/Z玻色子高度增强导致的衰变产物合并成单个fat-jet特征来抑制背景。在ILC（√s=1 TeV）和CLIC（√s=1.5 TeV）上执行全面的蒙特卡罗模拟，以积分亮度和混合参数sinθ_L为变量确定2σ排除和5σ发现边界。

Result: 未来e⁺e⁻对撞机可通过这些通道有效探测第一代单态VLL场景。1 TeV ILC可探测质量高达900 GeV的VLL；1.5 TeV CLIC将探测范围延伸至1400 GeV，超越了强子对撞机的现有极限，并对电弱精密测量的约束形成补充。

Conclusion: 未来正负电子对撞机在探测第一代单态矢量轻子方面具有显著优势，其探测能力覆盖更高质量区间，为超越标准模型的新物理搜索提供了有前景的平台，与现有实验约束共同完善了对VLL参数空间的覆盖。

Abstract: We study the single production of first-generation weak-isosinglet vector-like leptons (VLLs) at future $e^+e^-$ colliders, considering the channels $e^+e^- \to e^{\pm}E^{\mp}$ with $E^{\pm} \to W^{\pm}ν_e$ and $E^{\pm} \to e^{\pm}Z$. For the heavy VLL masses under consideration, the decay products of the highly boosted $W$ and $Z$ bosons merge into a single fat-jet, providing a powerful handle for signal identification and background suppression. A comprehensive Monte Carlo simulation is carried out at $\sqrt{s}=1$ TeV at the International Linear Collider (ILC) and 1.5 TeV at the Compact Linear Collider (CLIC). The $2σ$ exclusion and $5σ$ discovery reaches are determined as functions of the integrated luminosity and the mixing parameter $\sinθ_L$ for representative benchmark masses. Our results show that future $e^+e^-$ colliders can effectively probe the first-generation singlet VLL scenario through these channels. The 1 TeV ILC can probe masses up to 900 GeV, while the 1.5 TeV CLIC extends the reach to 1400 GeV, surpassing existing limits from hadron colliders and complementing constraints from electroweak precision measurements.

</details>


### [108] [Simultaneous production of a $W$ boson and a charmed hadron at the LHC in general-mass variable-flavour-number scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14268)
*Ville Alanko,Ilkka Helenius,Hannu Paukkunen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在一般质量变味数方案下，以微扰量子色动力学次领头阶研究了质子-质子碰撞中W±玻色子与粲强子的协同产生过程，利用该过程来约束奇异夸克部分子分布函数（PDF）。通过比较CT18A、MSHT20和NNPDF4.0三种PDF与ATLAS在13 TeV下的测量数据，发现强加零奇异数不对称性的CT18A与数据吻合最好，而允许非零奇异数不对称性的MSHT20和NNPDF4.0与数据存在更大张力。PDF重新加权方法进一步确认了对奇异数不对称性的敏感性，同时发现内禀粲夸克含量影响不显著，并对质子-铅碰撞中的测量可行性进行了探讨。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对奇异夸克PDF的约束一直是粒子物理中的重要问题，因为它直接影响弱相互作用的精确测量和标准模型检验。W±玻色子与粲强子协同产生过程对奇异夸克含量高度敏感，有望提供独立于Drell-Yan过程的新的PDF约束途径，特别是用于甄别奇异数不对称性（s≠s̄）是否存在。

Method: 在一般质量变味数方案（GM-VFNS）框架下，于次领头阶（NLO）水平计算pp→W±+粲强子的产生截面。通过构造带相反电荷粲介子（如D+与D-）的截面比，有效抵消非物理标度选择和碎裂函数带来的不确定性，使PDF不确定性成为主导。将该计算与CT18A、MSHT20、NNPDF4.0三种全局QCD拟合得到的PDF进行对比，结合ATLAS在√s=13 TeV的测量数据，并采用PDF重新加权（reweighting）方法进一步验证结果。

Result: 发现CT18A（零奇异数不对称性）与ATLAS数据一致最佳；MSHT20和NNPDF4.0（允许非零奇异数不对称性）与ATLAS数据存在更大张力。PDF重新加权方法确认了数据对奇异数不对称性的敏感性。质子的内禀粲夸克含量对该过程没有显著影响。在质子-铅碰撞中，利用高亮度LHC的预估探测器效率和投影亮度，这些过程虽然可观测，但对核PDF的约束能力相当有限。

Conclusion: W±+粲强子协同产生过程在GM-VFNS的NLO计算下可作为约束奇异夸克PDF的有效探针，且通过电荷比可系统压低非PDF不确定性。现有ATLAS数据倾向于支持零奇异数不对称性（如CT18A所假设），对允许非零s-s̄不对称性的PDF产生排斥。该过程对奇异数不对称性的精确约束能力已获验证，但对内禀粲含量和核PDF的敏感度有限。

Abstract: The simultaneous production of a $W^\pm$ boson and a charmed hadron in proton-proton collisions offers a potential probe for constraining the strange quark parton distribution functions (PDFs). We study these processes at next-to-leading order in perturbative Quantum Chromodynamics within the general-mass variable-flavor-number scheme. By considering ratios of cross-section between oppositely charged mesons, uncertainties associated with unphysical scale choices and fragmentation functions are shown to effectively cancel out, leaving the uncertainty originating from the PDFs as the dominant one. By comparing our calculations with CT18A, MSHT20 and NNPDF4.0 PDFs with the recent ATLAS measurement at $\sqrt{s} = 13\,{\rm TeV}$ we find that CT18A, which imposes zero strangeness asymmetry, agrees best with the data, while MSHT20 and NNPDF4.0, both of which allow for a non-zero strangeness asymmetry, exhibit greater tension with the ATLAS data. The sensitivity to the strangeness asymmetry is further confirmed by the PDF reweighting methods. We also study the impact of possible intrinsic charm content of the proton finding no significant sensitivity. Finally, we explore the possibility of measuring these processes in proton-lead collisions. With the estimated detector efficiencies and projected luminositites at the high-luminosity LHC, these processes should be visible, yet with a rather limited constraining power for nuclear PDFs.

</details>


### [109] [Nonstandard Solution for Anomaly Cancellation as Seesaw Neutrino Origin in the SM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14269)
*Zi-Yue Zou,Chia-Wei Liu,Zhong-Lv Huang,Xiao-Gang He*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于标准模型非阿贝尔表示下的15个手性费米子，发现异常消除存在非标准解。该解中奇异夸克和奇异轻子的超荷为零，奇异夸克单态带相反超荷。最小模型给出秩二跷跷板机制，预测无中微子双β衰变的有效质量，并讨论了直接SM实现和SU(2)_{L'}实现两种情形及其对奇异费米子质量和电量的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索标准模型中15个手性费米子在非阿贝尔表示下异常消除条件的非标准解，以扩展超出标准模型的物理可能性，并研究奇异粒子（奇异夸克和奇异轻子）的质量生成机制及可观测预言（如无中微子双β衰变）。

Method: 对固定SM非阿贝尔表示和任意超荷的15个手性费米子，求解异常消除条件找出非标准解；通过秩二跷跷板机制（最小跷跷板模型）生成中微子质量并数值预言无中微子双β衰变有效质量；分别考虑直接SM Higgs机制和独立SU(2)_{L'}规范对称实现来生成奇异费米子质量。

Result: 非标准解中奇异夸克双重态和奇异轻子双重态/单态具有零超荷，两个奇异夸克单态携带相反超荷±q。最小模型预言m_{ββ}为1.2–4.1 meV（正常顺序）或15.9–48.9 meV（倒转顺序）。直接SM实现中奇异夸克带电量为±1/2；SU(2)_{L'}实现中奇异夸克和轻子带电量±q，可允许奇异夸克质量在几TeV到10 TeV范围内且Yukawa耦合为O(1)。两种情形中最轻的奇异粒子均稳定，但合理选择电荷和质量可满足实验约束。

Conclusion: 该非标准解为超出标准模型的物理提供了新路径：奇异中性轻子自然充当重中微子，最小模型可实现最小跷跷板机制；两种实现方案（直接SM和SU(2)_{L'}）在可探测试验能标下允许奇异夸克和轻子的存在，并给出可检验的中微子质量预言。

Abstract: For fixed Standard Model (SM) non-Abelian representations of 15 chiral fermions with arbitrary hypercharges, anomaly cancellation admits the usual assignment and a distinct nonstandard solution. In the latter, the colored exotic quark and exotic lepton weak doublets and exotic lepton singlet have zero hypercharge, whereas the two colored exotic quark singlets carry opposite hypercharges $-q$ and $q$. The exotic neutral lepton singlets naturally play the role of the heavy neutrinos. The minimal model with two exotic lepton copies gives a rank-two seesaw, the minimal seesaw model, with one massless active neutrino and predicts $m_{ββ} = 1.2 \text{-} 4.1 \, \mathrm{meV}$ for normal ordering or $15.9\text{-}48.9 \,\mathrm{meV}$ for inverted ordering. In a direct SM realization, generating exotic quark masses through the SM Higgs mechanism forces the exotic quarks to carry electric charges $\pm 1/2$. A separate $\mathrm{SU}(2)_{L'}$ realization of the nonstandard solution can allow exotic quarks from several TeV to $10\,\mathrm{TeV}$ with order-one Yukawa couplings. In this model, the charged exotic quarks and leptons carry electric charges $\pm q$. In both cases, the lightest exotic quark and lepton are stable, but suitable choices of their charges and masses can satisfy experimental constraints.

</details>


### [110] [Pairton: Iterative Reconstruction of Short-Lived Particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14278)
*Andreas Hermansen,Chris Scheulen,Tobias Golling*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: Pairton是一个用于高能碰撞事件中短寿命粒子重建的迭代框架，将粒子重建形式化为图结构上的掩码预测过程，利用基于pairformer的架构和动态更新的成对表示，在全强子t\bar{t}衰变上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能碰撞事件中短寿命粒子的重建是实验粒子物理中的关键挑战，传统方法难以同时捕捉衰变产物的因子化结构、全局事件一致性以及粒子间的复杂条件依赖关系。

Method: 将粒子重建问题形式化为图结构上的掩码预测过程，学习与衰变产物因子化分解一致的条件分布；通过迭代预测邻接矩阵中的边来表示粒子衰变关系；采用pairformer架构，动态更新成对表示以融入全局事件一致性信息。

Result: 在全强子t\bar{t}衰变任务上展现了最先进（SOTA）的重建性能，证明了该方法的有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: Pairton提供了一种通用、灵活且可泛化的粒子重建范式，可方便地扩展到其他拓扑结构，为现代生成式建模与高能物理之间搭建了桥梁。

Abstract: We present Pairton, an iterative framework for reconstructing short-lived particles in high-energy collision events. By formulating particle reconstruction as a masked prediction process over graph structures, Pairton learns conditional distributions consistent with a factorised decomposition of decay products and iteratively predicts edges in the adjacency matrix representing particle decay relationships. Leveraging a pairformer-based architecture with dynamically updated pairwise representations, our method incorporates global event consistency. We demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on fully hadronic $t\bar{t}$ decays. Pairton provides a general, flexible paradigm for particle reconstruction and can be readily extended to other topologies, bridging ideas from modern generative modelling and high-energy physics.

</details>


### [111] [Enhanced Three-Particle Contribution to Electroweak Penguin $B$-Meson Decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14341)
*Yong-Kang Huang,Yu-Ming Wang,Xue-Chen Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次计算了罕见b→{s,d}ℓ⁺ℓ⁻衰变在次领头阶的twist修正，其中包含了三粒子B介子分布振幅在次次领头阶精度的贡献（采用软共线有效理论），并整合了多项NNLO QCD修正，为电弱企鹅B→{K,π}ℓ⁺ℓ⁻衰变提供了改进的场论预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 这是完整因子化分析重夸克展开领先阶强子矩阵元所缺失的最后一项关键成分，补齐后可获得更精确的理论预言。

Method: 采用软共线有效理论（SCET），计算三粒子B介子分布振幅在NLO精度的次领头twist修正；同时纳入 spectator-scattering 振幅和弱湮灭拓扑的各项NNLO QCD修正。

Result: 给出了电弱企鹅B→{K,π}ℓ⁺ℓ⁻衰变中具有物理意义的可观测量（如微分分支比、前后不对称等）的改进场论预言。

Conclusion: 该工作补齐了领先幂次因子化框架的最后缺失环节，显著提升了稀有B介子衰变理论预言的精度，对探测新物理具有重要意义。

Abstract: We compute for the first time the subleading twist correction to the exclusive rare $b \to \left \{s, d \right \} \ell^{+} \ell^{-}$ decays from the three-particle $B$-meson distribution amplitude at next-to-leading order accuracy by employing the soft-collinear effective theory. This constitutes the last missing ingredient for the complete factorization analysis of the hadronic matrix element of the weak effective Hamiltonian at leading power in the heavy quark expansion. Incorporating further a variety of the next-to-next-to-leading-order QCD corrections to the spectator-scattering amplitude and the weak annihilation topology, we then present the improved field-theoretic predictions for phenomenologically interesting observables in the electroweak penguin $B \to \left \{K, π\right \} \ell^{+} \ell^{-}$ decays.

</details>


### [112] [Pion and kaon D terms from holographic QCD and coupled-channel dispersion relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14347)
*Zhibo Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过将全息张量形状因子与手征-色散迹表示相结合，研究了π介子和K介子的D项（张量引力形状因子相关的标量形状因子）。利用前向Ward恒等式和弯曲时空SU(3)手征微扰理论确定原点处的归一化与斜率，并通过基于经验散射振幅的双通道ππ/K\bar K Muskhelishvili-Omnès解延伸到类空动量区域。在手征极限下，迹的显式夸克质量响应饱和给出D_π(0)=-1/3，而软π定理给出D_π(0)=-1。在物理质量下，SU(3)破缺区分π和K的前向值，K的D项在低Q²区域比π的负值更小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 介子D项是描述强子内部机械性质（压力、剪切力分布）的关键物理量，但目前实验和格点QCD对D项的精确测量仍面临挑战。本文旨在通过结合全息QCD与手征色散方法为π和K介子的D项提供一套精确的低能理论预言，作为未来格点QCD研究张量和标量通道的参考基准。

Method: 本文采用混合方法：(1) 利用精确迹恒等式将D项与张量引力形状因子A_M(t)和总标量迹Θ_M(t)联系起来；(2) 结合全息张量形状因子（描述高阶行为）与手征-色散迹表示（描述低能行为）；(3) 通过前向Ward恒等式和弯曲时空SU(3)手征微扰理论确定原点归一化与斜率；(4) 构建基于经验散射振幅的双通道ππ/K\bar K Muskhelishvili-Omnès方程求解，实现从类时到手征类空动量的解析延拓。

Result: 在手征极限下，迹的归一化显式夸克质量响应给出了D_π(0)=-1/3，而软π定理给出D_π(0)=-1，两者之差分离了显式质量贡献与剩余手征标量响应。在物理质量下，SU(3)破缺在手征匹配中区分了π和K的前向值，双通道重散射控制它们偏离原点的演化。在所考虑的低Q²类空区域，K介子D项比π介子D项负值更小，且此差异随Q²增大而缩小。结果与可用格点QCD数据进行了比较。

Conclusion: 本文为π和K介子的D项提供了结合全息与手征色散方法的综合理论预言，系统分析了手征极限行为、SU(3)破缺效应和耦合通道重散射的影响，为未来格点QCD在张量和标量通道的研究提供了重要的低能参考基准。

Abstract: For a spin-zero meson, the exact trace identity relates the D term to the tensor gravitational form factor $A_M(t)$ and the total scalar trace $Θ_M(t)$. We study the pion and kaon D terms by combining a holographic tensor form factor with a chiral-dispersive representation of the trace. Its normalization and slope at the origin are obtained from the forward Ward identity and curved-space SU(3) chiral perturbation theory. A two-channel $ππ/K\bar K$ Muskhelishvili--Omnès solution constructed from empirical scattering amplitudes describes the continuation to spacelike momentum. Available lattice-QCD results are shown for comparison. In the chiral limit, saturation of the trace by the normalized explicit quark-mass response gives $D_π(0)=-1/3$, while the soft-pion theorem gives $D_π(0)=-1$. This comparison separates the explicit-mass contribution from the remaining chiral scalar response. At physical masses, SU(3) breaking in the chiral matching distinguishes the pion and kaon forward values, and coupled-channel rescattering governs their evolution away from the origin. The kaon D term is less negative than the pion result in the low-$Q^2$ spacelike region considered, with the separation decreasing as $Q^2$ increases. These form factors provide a low-energy reference for future lattice studies of the tensor and scalar channels.

</details>


### [113] [Jet functions for next-to-leading power factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14382)
*Robin van Bijleveld,Jaco ter Hoeve,Eric Laenen,Coenraad Marinissen,Leonardo Vernazza,Guoxing Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在部分子阈值附近、阈值变量1-z下次前导幂（NLP）阶散射过程的分形化。作者回顾了SCET和直接QCD方法中幂次压低贡献的一般结构，讨论了NLP喷流函数作为QCD中规范不变的算子矩阵元的定义，并通过方法区（method of regions）在单圈和双圈水平上验证了该分形化公式，最后指出了NLP对数系统重求和所面临的两个挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现对NLP阶对数（次前导幂对数）的系统性重求和，需要建立严格的分形化框架。此前相关工作主要集中在LP（前导幂）阶，而NLP阶由于幂次压低贡献的结构更为复杂，且端点发散等问题尚未完全解决，因此需要系统的理论研究与验证。

Method: 本文结合两种互补的方法：（1）Soft-Collinear有效理论（SCET）框架，用于描述阈附近时软-共线模的分离；（2）直接QCD方法，用于交叉验证。作者将NLP喷流函数定义为QCD中规范不变的算子矩阵元，并利用method of regions在大质量电磁形状因子的极限m^2<<s下于单圈和双圈水平显式验证分形化公式。

Result: 作者在单圈和双圈水平上显式验证了所提出的NLP分形化公式在大质量电磁形状因子中的正确性，从而为NLP阶分形化提供了具体的受控检验。同时识别出两个关键难点：一是SCET卷积中的端点发散处理，二是将喷流函数推广到可描述任意数目软胶子发射的辐射过程——后者是实现指数化的最后缺失环节，因为纯软扇区已通过replica trick以广义webs的形式被理解。

Conclusion: 本文建立了NLP阶分形化的严格框架并给出了验证，但系统性的NLP对数重求和仍需解决两个遗留问题：SCET中的端点发散问题，以及NLP喷流函数向多重软胶子辐射过程的推广。

Abstract: We discuss the factorization of scattering processes near partonic threshold at next-to-leading power (NLP) in the threshold variable $1-z$, with $z \equiv q^2/\hat{s}$. We review the general structure of power-suppressed contributions both in Soft-Collinear Effective Theory (SCET) and in a direct QCD approach, and discuss the definition of NLP jet functions as gauge-invariant operator matrix elements in QCD. As a controlled check of the resulting factorization formula, we verify it explicitly at one and two loops for the massive electromagnetic form factor in the limit $m^2 \ll s$, using the method of regions. We conclude by outlining the two challenges that remain for a systematic resummation of NLP logarithms: the treatment of endpoint divergences in SCET convolutions, and the extension of jet functions to radiative processes capable of describing an arbitrary number of soft-gluon emissions -- the latter being the last missing ingredient for exponentiation, given that the purely soft sector is already understood in terms of generalised webs via the replica trick.

</details>


### [114] [NNLO QCD corrections to $WWγ$ production at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14419)
*Paolo Garbarino,Massimiliano Grazzini,Stefan Kallweit*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了WWγ产生的QCD次次领头阶（NNLO）辐射修正，包含W玻色子轻子衰变和离壳效应。两圈振幅的有限部分采用软光子近似，并以Zγ产生为参考过程验证近似精度。在13 TeV对撞能量下，NNLO修正使NLO结果增加约16%，微扰不确定度降至±4%水平，软光子近似引入的不确定度仅为千分之几。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三玻色子产生过程（如WWγ）是研究四次规范玻色子耦合（quartic gauge-boson couplings）的关键渠道。精确的理论预言（尤其是高阶QCD修正）对于在LHC上从实验测量中提取新物理信号至关重要，因此在NNLO精度上计算WWγ产生截面具有重要物理意义。

Method: 在NNLO QCD精度下计算WWγ产生的辐射修正。两圈振幅的有限部分采用软光子近似处理，完全包含W玻色子的轻子衰变和离壳效应。以Zγ产生为基准过程（具有精确的两圈振幅）来校准近似方法并给出保守误差估计，然后将该方法应用于WWγ产生。

Result: 在质心能量√s=13 TeV和典型选择截断条件下，NNLO修正使NLO结果提升约16%；微扰不确定度从NLO水平降至±4%量级。软光子近似引入的不确定度在千分之几水平，在膜截面和主要微分分布中均远小于残余微扰不确定度，表明该近似方法足够精确。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在NNLO QCD精度上提供了WWγ产生的完整理论预言，证实了软光子近似方法的有效性和可靠性。NNLO修正显著减小了理论不确定度，为LHC上三玻色子产生过程的研究和四次规范玻色子耦合的精确测量提供了重要的理论基础。

Abstract: Triboson production processes are crucial to study quartic gauge-boson couplings. We present the computation of the radiative corrections to $WWγ$ production at the next-to-next-to-leading order (NNLO) in QCD. The leptonic decays of the $W$ bosons and off-shell effects are fully included. The calculation is exact, apart from the finite part of the two-loop amplitudes, which is evaluated in a soft-photon approximation. We validate our approach by using $Zγ$ production as a reference process, where we take advantage of the availability of the exact two-loop amplitudes to derive a conservative error estimate for our approximation, and then apply it to $WWγ$ production. For typical selection cuts, at the centre-of-mass energy $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV, the NNLO corrections increase the next-to-leading order (NLO) result by about $16\%$, and the perturbative uncertainties are reduced to the $\pm4\%$ level. The uncertainty from the soft approximation turns out to be at the few per mille level, largely subdominant compared to the residual perturbative uncertainties, both for the fiducial cross section and the most relevant differential distributions.

</details>


### [115] [Understanding the enhanced $φη^{\prime}$ decay mode of the $φ(2170)$ through strange-meson loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14440)
*Qin-Song Zhou,Jun-Zhang Wang,Dan Guo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过同时分析e+e-→φη和e+e-→φη′两个道截面，研究了激发态奇异偶素φ(2170)的性质。结果表明，奇异介子圈的长程动力学机制能自然解释φη′/φη强度比异常偏大的谜题，且高精度φη数据支持在2.15 GeV附近存在一个宽度约25 MeV的额外窄矢量态，可能为轻矢量区的奇特强子候选者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: φ(2170)的奇异偶素性质一直存有争议。近期e+e-→φη(′)测量揭示了一个显著的谜题：尽管φη′道存在强烈的相空间压低，但宽φ(2170)结构出现在φη′道而非φη道，导致φη′衰变分数异常偏大。作者旨在解释这一谜题，并检验是否存在其他物理贡献。

Method: 在激发态奇异偶素框架下对e+e-→φη和e+e-→φη′两个道的截面进行联合拟合。同时包含两类振幅：来自真空夸克对产生的短程贡献，以及由奇异介子圈介导的长程跃迁贡献。通过比较两种机制预言的φη′/φη强度比来判断哪种机制更符合数据。

Result: 短程机制预言的φη′/φη强度比过小而无法解释数据；而奇异介子圈因K(∗)K(∗)η(′)顶点处的SU(3)味道因子天然压低φη道的跃迁，从而增大了该比值，能较好描述数据。此外，尽管已包含激发态奇异偶素贡献，高精度φη数据仍倾向于存在一个位于约2.15 GeV、宽度约25 MeV的额外窄矢量态。测试了三种比值Γ(Y→φη)/Γ(Y→φη′)=0.25,1,4作为拟合输入，三种情况均能给出同等好的截面描述，当前数据无法以此区分该窄态的内部构型。

Conclusion: 长程动力学（奇异介子圈）在激发态矢量奇异偶素的隐奇异度衰变中起重要作用；若约2.15 GeV处的窄矢量态被实验确认，将是轻矢量区一个有前景的奇特强子候选者。但现有数据尚不足以通过φη/φη′分支比来甄别该窄态的内部结构。

Abstract: The nature of the strangeonium-like $φ(2170)$ remains controversial. Recent measurements of $e^+e^-\toφη^{(\prime)}$ reveal a striking puzzle: a broad $φ(2170)$-like structure appears in the $φη^\prime$ channel but not in $φη$, despite the strong phase-space suppression of $φη^\prime$. We investigate this unexpectedly large $φη^\prime$ decay fraction within the excited-strangeonium assignment. A combined analysis of the $e^+e^-\toφη$ and $e^+e^-\toφη^\prime$ cross sections is performed by including short-distance amplitudes from vacuum quark-pair creation and long-distance transitions mediated by strange-meson loops. The short-distance mechanism predicts too small a $φη^\prime/φη$ strength ratio to explain the data. In contrast, strange-meson loops naturally enhance this ratio because the SU(3)-flavor factor at the $K^{(*)}K^{(*)}η^{(\prime)}$ vertex suppresses the transition to $φη$ relative to $φη^\prime$. The resulting overall description of both cross sections supports an important role for long-distance dynamics in hidden-strangeness decays of excited vector strangeonia. More intriguingly, although the excited strangeonium contributions have been included, the current high-precision $φη$ data still favor the existence of an extra narrow vector state near $2.15~\mathrm{GeV}$ with a width of about $25~\mathrm{MeV}$. If confirmed, it would be a promising exotic-hadron candidate in the light-vector sector. Furthermore, we test $Γ(Y\toφη)/Γ(Y\toφη^\prime)=0.25$, $1$, and $4$ as fit inputs and obtain similarly good descriptions of the cross sections in all three cases. The present data therefore do not allow this ratio to discriminate among different internal configurations of the narrow state.

</details>


### [116] [Improving lepton flavour universality tests with $K_L$ decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14474)
*G. D'Ambrosio,A. M. Iyer,F. Mahmoudi,S. Neshatpour*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对稀有K介子衰变进行全局分析，结合最新实验结果和更新后的标准模型预测，发现拟合最优解接近标准模型，同时存在一个具有现象学意义的次局部最小值。文章定义了与这两个区域相关的基准场景，并研究了NA62和KOTO-II实验对新物理参数空间的预期灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀有K介子衰变是探测标准模型味结构及可能的新物理的敏感探针。随着K+→π+νν̄的最新测量结果和K+→π+ℓ+ℓ−中轻子味普适性观测量的出现，需要对这些新数据进行全局分析，以评估当前实验与理论状态下的新物理参数空间，并前瞻性地评估未来实验的判别能力。

Method: 作者进行了全局拟合分析，纳入最近的实验测量结果和更新后的标准模型理论预测，包括K+→π+νν̄的最新测量以及K+→π+ℓ+ℓ−中的轻子味普适性观测量。基于拟合中出现的两个相关区域定义基准场景，进一步考虑NA62对K+→π+νν̄和轻子味普适性观测量的预测测量，以及KOTO-II对KL→π0νν̄、KL→π0e+e−和KL→π0μ+μ−的预测测量，评估其对新物理参数空间的探测灵敏度与判别能力。

Result: 拟合结果表明最佳拟合点接近标准模型预测，但存在第二个现象学相关的局部最小值。研究发现KOTO-II在探测和区分可行的新物理场景方面具有显著潜力，而NA62提供互补的灵敏度覆盖。

Conclusion: KOTO-II与NA62在未来稀有K介子衰变测量中可形成互补，前者具有较强的场景判别能力，后者提供补充灵敏度，两者结合可有效约束或发现新物理信号。

Abstract: Rare kaon decays provide sensitive probes of the flavour structure of the Standard Model and of possible new physics. We perform a global analysis incorporating recent experimental results and updated Standard Model predictions, including the latest measurement of $K^+ \to π^+ ν\barν$ and lepton flavour universality observables in $K^+ \to π^+ \ell^+\ell^-$. The fit favours a best-fit point close to the Standard Model, while a second local minimum remains phenomenologically relevant. We define benchmark scenarios associated with these two regions and investigate the prospective sensitivity of NA62 and KOTO-II to the new physics parameter space. We consider projected measurements of $K^+ \to π^+ ν\barν$ and lepton flavour universality observables in $K^+ \to π^+ \ell^+\ell^-$ at NA62, and of $K_L \to π^0 ν\barν$, $K_L \to π^0 e^+e^-$, and $K_L \to π^0 μ^+μ^-$ at KOTO-II. We find that KOTO-II has significant potential to probe and discriminate between the viable new physics scenarios, with NA62 providing complementary sensitivity.

</details>


### [117] [Vector-Like Fermions at FCC-ee: NLO Higgs-Strahlung Signatures and Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14538)
*Carlo Marzo,Vinicius Padovani,Daniele Rizzo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了矢量样费米子（VLFs）对Higgs-strahlung截面（σ(ZH)）的单圈效应，这是未来e⁺e⁻ Higgs工厂（如FCC-ee）的核心精度观测量。在四个SM扩展基准模型下（两个Yukawa驱动、两个混合驱动），采用on-shell重整化方案并考虑RG跑动下的微扰性边界，作者发现Yukawa驱动的场景中，当前LHC仍允许的相当大参数空间可以产生达到或超过FCC-ee千分之一灵敏度的σ(ZH)偏移；而混合驱动场景中，现有电弱精确约束已排除可观测效应。结论是VL Yukawa耦合比与SM费米子的残余混合更适合作为未来e⁺e⁻对撞机Higgs-strahlung精确测量的探测目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FCC-ee等未来e⁺e⁻ Higgs工厂将以前所未有的精度（千分位量级）测量e⁺e⁻→ZH过程的截面。在SM以外的扩展中，矢量样费米子（VLF）是常见的新物理候选，但其对Higgs-strahlung的单圈辐射修正是否可被未来实验探测尚未进行系统研究。作者旨在评估VLF通过Yukawa耦合与混合两种机制对σ(ZH)的贡献，判断其作为新物理探针的可行性与前景。

Method: 在on-shell重整化方案下，对四个基准模型进行计算：(1) VL夸克双重态+单态伙伴；(2) VL轻子双重态+单态伙伴，两类均通过纯内部Yukawa耦合与Higgs相互作用；(3) 单个VL单态与第三代带电轻子直接混合；(4) 单个VL单态与第三代中微子混合。计算了Higgs-strahlung振幅中的普适自能贡献与非普适顶点贡献的单圈修正，并结合RG跑动施加微扰性约束。作为交叉验证，同时计算了斜参数S、T以及H→γγ衰变率。最后将结果与当前LHC的限制和FCC-ee预期的千分之一灵敏度进行对比。

Result: 在Yukawa驱动的两个场景中，当前LHC搜索仍允许的参数空间内有相当大比例可产生达到或超过FCC-ee千分之一灵敏度（per-mille）的σ(ZH)偏移，表明Higgs-strahlung可作为这些内部Yukawa耦合的辐射探针。在混合驱动的两个场景中，现有的电弱精确测量对相关混合角的约束已经排除了任何可观测的效应（尽管作者完整刻画了底层圈动力学供未来参考）。综合结果表明，VL Yukawa耦合而非与SM费米子的残余混合才是未来e⁺e⁻对撞机Higgs-strahlung精确测量更有前景的目标。

Conclusion: 矢量样费米子的内部Yukawa耦合为其与Higgs玻色子的相互作用提供了可被FCC-ee级别精度仪器探测的辐射信号，而受制于现有电弱精确约束，VLF与SM费米子的混合在σ(ZH)中不足以产生可观测偏移。因此，未来的Higgs-strahlung精确测量应当将VLF的Yukawa耦合定位为主要探测目标，这为在FCC-ee上寻找VLF新物理指明了方向。

Abstract: We compute the one-loop effects of vector-like fermions (VLFs) on the Higgs-strahlung cross-section, the flagship precision observable of future $e^+e^-$ Higgs factories such as FCC-ee. We consider four benchmark extensions of the Standard Model (SM): two in which a VL quark (VLQ) or VL lepton (VLL) doublet, together with its singlet partners, couples to the Higgs boson through purely internal Yukawa interactions, and two in which a single VL singlet mixes directly with the third-generation charged lepton or neutrino. Working throughout in the on-shell renormalization scheme and imposing perturbativity bounds on the relevant couplings under renormalization-group (RG) running, we compute both the universal self-energy and the non-universal vertex contributions to the Higgs-strahlung amplitude at one loop, together with the oblique parameters $S$ and $T$ and the $H\toγγ$ rate as complementary cross-checks. In the two Yukawa-driven scenarios, we find that a sizable fraction of the parameter space still allowed by current LHC searches produces shifts in $σ(ZH)$ at or above the per-mille sensitivity envisioned for FCC-ee, offering a radiative probe of these couplings. In the two mixing-driven scenarios, existing electroweak-precision bounds on the relevant mixing angles already preclude an observable effect, although we characterize the underlying loop dynamics in full generality for future reference. These results identify the VL Yukawa couplings, rather than any residual mixing with the SM fermions, as the more promising target for Higgs-strahlung precision measurements at a future $e^+e^-$ collider.

</details>


### [118] [Blazar Boosted Dark Matter in IceCube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14549)
*Alberto M. Gago,Jaime Hoefken Zink,Joel Jones-Pérez,Gabriel D. Zapata*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了冰立方（IceCube）对耀变体加速暗物质的探测灵敏度。作者采用含有质量矢量介子耦合到夸克的费米子暗物质模型，基于324个耀变体样本和保守的暗物质尖峰轮廓，计算了扩散通量，并包括弹性散射、共振单π产生和深度非弹性等暗物质-核子散射贡献。研究显示该策略在暗物质质量低于约1 GeV时优于传统直接探测实验，且信号主要由深度非弹性散射主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索亚GeV量级暗物质探测的新途径。传统直接探测实验对低质量暗物质的灵敏度有限，而通过耀变体加速暗物质并在IceCube中探测其产生的中微子信号，可能为暗物质研究提供互补且更灵敏的手段，尤其是在暗物质-核子散射的弹性到非弹性区域过渡问题上。

Method: 采用包含质量矢量介子作为媒介子的费米子暗物质模型。利用324个具有多波段观测质子谱的耀变体样本，结合围绕中心超大质量黑洞的保守暗物质尖峰轮廓，计算耀变体加速暗物质的扩散通量。散射截面的计算涵盖弹性散射、共振单π产生和深度非弹性三种贡献，并特别强调共振单π产生通道以平滑覆盖弹性到深度非弹性区域的过渡。同时考虑中微子在地球传播过程中的衰减效应，利用IceCube中微子数据对模型参数空间施加约束。

Result: 该探测策略在暗物质质量低于约1 GeV时能够超越传统直接探测实验的灵敏度。信号主要由深度非弹性散射主导，因此对较重媒介子更为敏感。共振过程会导致事件率降低，在实验阈值附近最多减少约9%。

Conclusion: IceCube通过观测耀变体加速暗物质，构成了探测亚GeV暗物质情景的有力工具，为暗物质研究提供了新的探测窗口。

Abstract: We study the sensitivity of IceCube to blazar-boosted dark matter in a fermionic dark matter model with a massive vector mediator coupling to quarks. To this aim, we compute the diffuse flux arising from a sample of 324 blazars with proton spectra inferred from multiwavelength observations, adopting conservative dark matter spike profiles around the central supermassive black holes and consistently accounting for attenuation effects during propagation through the Earth. The dark matter-nucleon scattering cross section is evaluated by including elastic, resonant single pion production, and deep inelastic contributions, with particular emphasis on resonant single-pion production channels in order to smoothly cover the transition between the elastic and deep inelastic regimes. Using IceCube neutrino data, we derive constraints on the parameter space of the model and show that this detection strategy can surpass the sensitivity of conventional direct-detection experiments for dark matter masses below $\sim 1$ GeV. We find that the signal is dominated by deep inelastic scattering and is therefore more sensitive to comparatively heavy mediators, while resonance processes provide a reduction of the event rate, reaching up to about $9\%$ near the experimental threshold. Our results demonstrate that IceCube constitutes a powerful probe of sub-GeV dark matter scenarios through the observation of blazar-boosted dark matter.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Algebraic curves admitting automorphism groups of large prime square order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13768)
*Nazar Arakelian,Diego Kian*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文研究代数闭域（任意特征）上曲线的自同构群，给出了其阶为素数$\ell$的平方（即$\ell^2$）时的上界估计，并对达到上界的曲线族在驯顺与野性两种情形下进行分类，还给出了达到最高界曲线群的完全自同构群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在代数几何中，理解曲线自同构群的阶数是经典且核心的问题。已有的结果多为素数阶自同构或一般阶的研究，但对于阶恰为素数平方（$\ell^2$）的情况，尤其在任意特征（包含野性情形）下缺乏系统的上界估计与分类结果。本文旨在填补这一空白，为任意特征下曲线自同构群的阶为平方素数的情况提供精确的界并完成分类。

Method: 本文利用代数闭域上曲线自同构理论中的经典工具（如Hurwitz公式、有理函数域的子域对应等），针对驯顺和野性两种分支情形分别建立素数$\ell$的阶$\ell^2$自同构群存在的必要条件，推导出$\ell$的数值上界。随后通过构造或识别达到上界的曲线族，对它们进行分类，并利用群论方法（如半直积、群扩张等）确定这些曲线的完全自同构群。

Result: 给出了阶为$\ell^2$的曲线自同构群中素数$\ell$的明确上界；在驯顺与野性两种情形下分别对达到上界的曲线族完成了完整分类；并刻画了这些达到最高界的曲线的完全自同构群的结构。

Conclusion: 本文在任意特征（包括野性）下完善了阶为素数平方的自同构群的上界理论，并通过对达到上界曲线的分类与完全自同构群的确定，为此类曲线提供了完整的结构信息，进一步推动了曲线自同构群一般理论的发展。

Abstract: Let $\mathbb{K}$ denote an algebraically closed field of arbitrary characteristic. In this paper we provide bounds for the size of a prime $\ell$ for which there are curves defined over $\mathbb{K}$ admitting automorphism groups of order $\ell^2$. In addition, we also give a classification of the families of curves attaining the upper bounds for $\ell$ in both tame and wild case. Finally, we present the full automorphism groups of the curves attaining the highest bounds.

</details>


### [120] [The Lie algebra of polynomial vector fields on the affine space with constant divergence is $1.5$-generated](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13798)
*Ivan Beldiev,Gleb Pogudin*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了仿射空间上具有常散度的多项式向量场李代数是1.5-生成的，同时证明了复仿射空间上所有多项式向量场的李代数可由两个完全可积元素生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究李代数的生成性质，特别是1.5-生成这一概念——即每个非零元素都可以补全为一个二元素生成集，这比传统的有限生成性质更强，有助于深入理解李代数的代数结构。

Method: 通过构造性的方法，证明具有常散度的多项式向量场李代数满足1.5-生成性质，并构造性地证明多项式向量场全李代数存在由两个完全可积元素构成的生成集。

Result: 1. 仿射空间上具有常散度的多项式向量场李代数是1.5-生成的；2. 复仿射空间上所有多项式向量场的李代数可由两个完全可积元素生成。

Conclusion: 这两个结果均揭示了多项式向量场李代数在生成性质上的深层结构，一方面扩展了1.5-生成李代数的类族，另一方面为完全可积生成提供了新的实例。

Abstract: A Lie algebra is said to be $1.5$-generated if every nonzero element can be completed to a two-element generating set. We prove that the Lie algebra of polynomial vector fields with constant divergence on the affine space is $1.5$-generated. We also show that the Lie algebra of all polynomial vector fields on the complex affine space is generated by two completely integrable elements.

</details>


### [121] [Twisted forms of classical hypersurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13896)
*Alvaro Liendo,Matías Vásquez*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了实数域和有限域上三大经典光滑超曲面簇（Fermat、Delsarte 和 Klein 超曲面）的扭曲形式计数问题。主要工具是一个计数公式，用于计算 Galois 上同调集，该公式适用于自同构群为对角阿贝尔群与单项置换群的半直积的光滑超曲面。为将其应用于有限域，作者将 Oguiso-Yu 的微分方法推广到正特征，并在显式算术条件下计算了正特征代数闭域上这三类超曲面的自同构群。在实数域上的计数结果恢复了 Sasaki 关于 Fermat 超曲面实形式的最近定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭曲形式（twisted forms）是算术几何中分类代数簇的基本问题，而具有大自同构群的超曲面（如 Fermat、Delsarte、Klein 簇）在划分上具有特殊的重要性。此前对实数域和有限域上这些经典超曲面的扭曲形式计数缺乏统一的系统性方法，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 建立了一个 Galois 上同调集的计数公式，适用于自同构群为半直积（对角阿贝尔群 ⋉ 单项置换群）的光滑超曲面；2) 将 Oguiso-Yu 的微分方法推广到正特征，以处理有限域情形；3) 在 p 满足显式算术条件下，计算正特征代数闭域上 Fermat、Delsarte 和 Klein 超曲面的自同构群，进而应用计数公式。

Result: 完成了实数域和有限域上三类经典超曲面的扭曲形式计数，并在正特征下给出了自同构群的完整计算。实数域上的计数结果与 Sasaki 关于 Fermat 超曲面实形式的定理一致，验证了方法的正确性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个统一且有效的框架来计数具有半直积型自同构群的光滑超曲面的扭曲形式，并在实数域和有限域上完整解决了 Fermat、Delsarte 和 Klein 三类经典超曲面的扭曲形式分类问题。

Abstract: We count the twisted forms, over the field of real numbers and over finite fields, of the three classical families of smooth hypersurfaces with large automorphism group: the Fermat, Delsarte and Klein hypersurfaces. Our main tool is a counting formula for the Galois cohomology set of a smooth hypersurface whose automorphism group is the semidirect product of a diagonal abelian group and a group of permutations of the monomials of its defining equation. In order to apply this formula over finite fields, we extend the differential method of Oguiso and Yu to positive characteristic, and we compute the automorphism groups of the Fermat, Delsarte and Klein hypersurfaces over algebraically closed fields of positive characteristic under explicit arithmetic conditions on p. Over the reals, our count recovers a recent theorem of Sasaki on the real forms of Fermat hypersurfaces.

</details>


### [122] [Rank-two weak Fano bundles on a four-dimensional quadric hypersurface $Q^4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13916)
*Yuta Takahashi*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文对四维光滑超二次曲面 $Q^4$ 上的秩 2 弱 Fano 向量丛进行了完全分类。结果表明，在扭转一个线丛的意义下，这类丛只能是分裂丛、旋量丛或在 [APW94] 中构造的具特定陈类 $c_1=-1$、$c_2=(1,1)$ 的稳定丛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱 Fano 向量丛在代数几何中具有重要地位，尤其是在研究 Fano 流形上的向量丛分类问题中。四维光滑超二次曲面 $Q^4$ 上的秩 2 向量丛分类是一个自然且有挑战性的问题，此前尚未有完整的分类结果。

Method: 通过分类 $Q^4$ 上秩 2 弱 Fano 向量丛，利用扭转线丛的技巧将问题简化，并结合陈类不变量以及已有文献 [APW94] 中的构造结果，给出系统性的分类论证。

Result: 在扭转意义下，$Q^4$ 上的秩 2 弱 Fano 向量丛仅有三类：分裂丛、旋量丛，以及 [APW94] 中构造的满足 $c_1=-1$、$c_2=(1,1)$ 的稳定丛。

Conclusion: 该分类完整刻画了四维光滑超二次曲面上秩 2 弱 Fano 向量丛的全体同构类，揭示了该问题仅有非常有限的几种结构类型，为后续相关研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We classify rank $2$ weak Fano bundles on a four-dimensional smooth quadric hypersurface $Q^4$. Up to twisting with a line bundle, such a bundle is either a split bundle, a spinor bundle, or one of the stable bundles with Chern classes $c_1=-1$ and $c_2=(1,1)$ constructed in [APW94].

</details>


### [123] [Scrollar invariants of singular curves on toric surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14080)
*Karl Christ,Xiang He,Ilya Tyomkin*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究环面曲面上一般整曲线的scrollar不变量。对于一大类环面曲面，确定了固定几何亏格的一般整曲线在单项式投影下诱导的scrollar不变量，推广了Castryck-Cools关于光滑曲线（特征零）的组合公式。文章描述了一种对于任何环面曲面都预期的行为，但也提供了在相应Severi簇的某些不可约分量上该行为失败的例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Castryck和Cools在特征零下针对光滑曲线建立的scrollar不变量组合公式推广到更一般的情形（一般整曲线、任意特征、更广的环面曲面类别），并探究该公式的适用范围与失效边界。

Method: 借助环面曲面的组合结构（如多边形的面数据），研究固定几何亏格的一般整曲线在单项式投影下诱导的从曲线正规化到射影直线的映射，确定其相关的scrollar不变量（即由该映射的Riemann-Roch向量空间所决定的数值不变量）。通过代数几何与组合方法推广原公式，并构造反例检验公式在Severi簇不同不可约分量上的有效性。

Result: 对于一大类环面曲面，确立了固定几何亏格的一般整曲线的scrollar不变量的组合计算公式；同时构造了具体例子表明该预期行为并非对所有环面曲面成立，即在某些Severi簇的不可约分量上公式失效。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了scrollar不变量组合计算的适用范围到一般整曲线情形，但揭示了该预期行为在一般环面曲面上的局限，说明公式的适用性依赖于Severi簇的分量结构。

Abstract: Given a curve on a toric surface, a monomial projection induces a map from the normalization of the curve to the projective line. We determine the associated scrollar invariants for general integral curves of fixed geometric genus for a large class of toric surfaces. This generalizes a combinatorial formula to calculate such scrollar invariants for smooth curves in characteristic zero due to Castryck and Cools. We describe an expected behaviour for any toric surface, but provide examples where this fails at least on some irreducible component of the corresponding Severi variety.

</details>


### [124] [The Hassett-Keel program via $Θ$-stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14099)
*Luca Tasin,Filippo Viviani*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了超越GIT方法中曲线的α-稳定性内在概念，证明其在9/11<α≤1范围内恢复Deligne-Mumford稳定性，并且α-半稳定轨迹包含在Hassett-Keel程序的已知模紧化中（α>2/3-ε）。还获得了光滑曲线新的α-稳定性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越GIT（Beyond GIT）方法为模空间提供了新的稳定性概念，但需要验证这些概念与经典模紧化之间的关系，以及其适用范围。

Method: 结合斜率不等式与退化定理：每个具有非节点奇点的Gorenstein曲线都可以等变退化（isotrivially degenerated）为带G_m作用的Gorenstein曲线。

Result: 对于9/11<α≤1，α-稳定性恢复Deligne-Mumford稳定性；对于α>2/3-ε，α-半稳定轨迹包含在Hassett-Keel程序的已知模紧化中。此外还得到了光滑曲线的新α-稳定性结果。

Conclusion: α-稳定性在特定阈值范围内与经典稳定性理论相容，这为Hassett-Keel程序提供了内在的模理论支撑，并扩展了对光滑曲线稳定性的理解。

Abstract: We study the intrinsic notion of $α$-stability for curves arising from the Beyond GIT approach to moduli spaces. We show that it recovers Deligne-Mumford stability for $9/11<α\le1$, and more generally that the $α$-semistable locus is contained in the known modular compactification of the Hassett-Keel proram for $α>2/3-\varepsilon$. As applications, we also obtain new $α$-stability results for smooth curves. Our approach combines slope inequalities with a degeneration theorem showing that every Gorenstein curve with a non-nodal singularity can be isotrivially degenerated to a Gorenstein curve with a $\mathbb G_m$-action.

</details>


### [125] [The alpha spectrum of K-polystable toric $\mathbb{Q}$-Fano varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14115)
*Xian Wu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文明确确定了n维K-多项式稳定环面Q-Fano簇的非等变α-不变量的数值谱，证明了其取值范围恰为有理数区间[1/(n+1), 1/2]，建立了完整的环面实现定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究K-多项式稳定环面Q-Fano簇的α-不变量的数值范围，回答并强化了Liu-Zhuang提出的问题，并细化了Liu-Zhu最近的构造结果。

Method: 通过环面几何的方法，明确计算非等变α-不变量的数值谱，系统刻画K-多项式稳定条件与α-不变量之间的联系。

Result: 证明了n维K-多项式稳定环面Q-Fano簇的α-不变量集合恰好等于有理数区间[1/(n+1), 1/2]，给出了完整的数值谱刻画。

Conclusion: 建立了环面Q-Fano簇α-不变量的完全实现定理，强化了已有问题并细化了先前结果，为K-稳定性与α-不变量的关系提供了精确的环面示例。

Abstract: We explicitly determine the numerical spectrum of the ordinary, non-equivariant $α$-invariant for $n$-dimensional K-polystable toric $\mathbb{Q}$-Fano varieties. Specifically, we prove that $\left\{α(X)\mid X\text{ is an }n\text{-dimensional K-polystable toric }\mathbb{Q}\text{-Fano variety}\right\}=\mathbb{Q}\cap\left[\frac{1}{n+1},\frac{1}{2}\right].$ This result establishes a complete toric realization theorem, which strengthens a question raised by Liu and Zhuang and refines the recent construction results of Liu and Zhu.

</details>


### [126] [The Quartic Hessian Conjecture in Dimension Four](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14217)
*Zixiang Ni*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了Hessian猜想在四变量四次多项式情形下的成立：若一个四次多项式具有不为零的常数Hessian行列式，则其梯度映射存在多项式逆。证明将齐次四次部分按Hesse定理分为三类（真三元四次、二元四次、线性形式的四次幂），每种类型分别通过不同次数的行列式方程将多项式约化为统一形式，随后独立于P的次数与顶部齐次部分证明该形式的所有常数Hessian多项式均有多项式梯度逆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hessian猜想是仿射代数几何与Jacobian猜想研究中的核心开放问题：在dimension≤3时成立、dimension≥5时反例存在、dimension 4仍为开放。作者通过攻克四变量四次情形的特例，为进一步解决维度四的完整猜想提供关键步骤和新的证明技术。

Method: （1）利用四维齐次Hesse定理，将零Hessian行列式的顶部齐次四次部分分类为三种穷举类型：真三元四次（具有非零三元Hessian）、真二元四次、线性形式的四次幂。（2）对每种类型分别由degree-seven/degree-six/degree-five行列式方程推导出三次部分在锥方向上的仿射线性结构，并引入常数方向引理。（3）将所有情况约化为统一形式f=P(x1,x2,x3)+x4Q(x1,x2,x3)+a x4²，其中deg Q≤2。（4）对a≠0的分支，通过Schur补从三维Hessian猜想降维获得结果；对a=0的分支，利用各向同性锥秩分析排除rank 2，以显式三角逆解决rank 1例外，并将rank 0约化为二维Hessian猜想。

Result: 证明了四变量四次情形的Hessian猜想成立，且所有约化类型均归结为统一形式；该形式下所有常数Hessian多项式均具有多项式梯度逆，涵盖a≠0（Schur补自三维情形）与a=0（秩分析排除rank 2、显式三角逆解决rank 1、归约至二维情形）两个分支。

Conclusion: 四变量四次情形下Hessian猜想成立。证明将问题系统约化为一个统一的标准形式，并通过Schur补与秩分析对分支分别处理，且在整个过程中保留了耦合的degree-six恒等式、不对固定二次形式分离分量，为未来解决完整dimension-4 Hessian猜想提供了可推广的框架。

Abstract: The Hessian conjecture asks whether a polynomial with nonzero constant Hessian determinant has a polynomial gradient inverse. It is known in dimensions at most three, false in dimensions at least five, and open in dimension four. We prove its four-variable quartic case.
  The top homogeneous part has zero Hessian determinant and, by the four-dimensional homogeneous Hesse theorem, is a cone. We divide its cone representative into three exhaustive types: a genuinely ternary quartic with nonzero ternary Hessian, a genuinely binary quartic, and a fourth power of a linear form. In the first type, the degree-seven determinant equation forces the cubic part to be affine-linear in the cone direction. In the binary type, the degree-six equation gives a constant null direction in a two-variable Hessian of the cubic part. In the unary type, the degree-five equation and a constant-direction lemma give the same conclusion. Every type therefore reduces to \[ f=P(x_1,x_2,x_3)+x_4Q(x_1,x_2,x_3)+a x_4^2, \qquad °Q\leq2. \] We prove, independently of the degree or top part of \(P\), that every constant-Hessian polynomial of this form has a polynomial gradient inverse. The branch \(a\ne0\) descends from the known three-dimensional Hessian conjecture after a Schur complement. When \(a=0\), an isotropic-cone rank analysis eliminates rank two, solves the rank-one exception by an explicit triangular inverse, and reduces rank zero to the two-dimensional Hessian conjecture. The coupled degree-six identity is retained throughout; no component with respect to a fixed quadratic form is separated.

</details>


### [127] [The cone theorem for effective fourfold pairs in characteristic $p>5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14236)
*Joe Waldron*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文假设所有与X双有理的log pair满足log分辨率猜想，在正特征和混合特征p>5的基础上，证明了射影log canonical、Q-factorial四维配对(X,Δ)的锥定理，其中K_X+Δ≡M≥0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在正特征和混合特征环境下，高维代数几何中的极小模型纲领（MMP）中核心的锥定理尚未完全建立。本文旨在将此结果推广到四维log canonical配对，并覆盖混合特征情形。

Method: 依赖log分辨率猜想的假设，采用类似于复代数几何中cone theorem的证明策略，将其适配到正特征p>5及混合特征的四维情形，结合K_X+Δ≡M≥0的条件进行论证。

Result: 在给定假设下，成功证明了射影log canonical、Q-factorial四维配对在正特征和混合特征p>5时的锥定理。

Conclusion: 该结果填补了正特征与混合特征下四维log canonical配对的锥定理空白，但依赖于log分辨率猜想作为先决条件。

Abstract: Assuming the log resolution conjecture for all log pairs birational to $X$, we prove the cone theorem for projective log canonical, $\mathbb{Q}$-factorial fourfold pairs $(X,Δ)$ such that $K_X +Δ\equiv M \geq 0$ over bases of positive and mixed characteristic $p > 5$.

</details>


### [128] [Estimation of calculation errors and resolution of singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14271)
*Victor Fadinger-Held,Daniel Windisch*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇解释性文章，介绍如何使用奇点消解和实对数临界阈值（RLCT）来量化数学错误（如中学生常见错误）的严重程度，并展示如何借助计算机代数软件进行计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在回答"数学错误到底有多严重"这一问题，将高中学数学中的常见错误（如"新生梦想"$(a+b)^2=a^2+b^2$）与代数几何中的奇点理论联系起来，提出了用RLCT作为衡量错误程度的数学工具。

Method: 引入实对数临界阈值（RLCT）作为量化错误的工具，利用奇点消解理论来计算该不变量；通过若干典型的中学数学错误案例（如特征2中的$(a+b)^2=a^2+b^2$、统计学中均值计算的错误）演示计算方法，并展示如何使用计算机代数软件辅助求解。

Result: 针对多个典型错误类计算了其RLCT值，展示了RLCT作为衡量"错误严重程度"的可行性，并证明了计算机代数软件在该问题上的实用性和有效性。

Conclusion: RLCT作为一个具有深刻代数几何背景（与极小模型程序相关）且同时连接贝叶斯统计与机器学习的数学不变量，可以自然地作为量化数学错误严重性的指标，为数学教育中发现的问题提供了一个严谨的数学框架。

Abstract: There are two possible answers to "$(a+b)^2 = a^2 + b^2$". Either "this is completely wrong" or "I assume, you are in characteristic $2$". If we go for the first answer, we could ask ourselves: "How wrong is it actually?" In this expository article, we introduce and use resolution of singularities and the real log canonical threshold (RLCT) as a measure for such errors. The RLCT is an invariant with connections to the minimal model program and, as recently discovered and investigated, to Bayesian statistics and machine learning. Inspired by typical mistakes from high school mathematics, we compute this invariant for several classes of examples, among them the error arising from the "freshman's dream" above and from a flawed computation of means in elementary statistics. In some examples, we highlight how a computer algebra software can be used in order to solve this problem.

</details>


### [129] [Weight decomposition for toroidal abelian fibrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14337)
*Younghan Bae,Jeremy Feusi,Aitor Iribar Lopez,Sam Molcho*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了有理数N乘法映射在环面秩至多为一的主极化阿贝尔簇退化族上的Chow群作用，及其与Fourier变换的交互关系。作为应用，计算了单位截面的类，证明了这类退化族通用族相对Chow motive的广义权分解，并完整确定了其tautological环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解主极化阿贝尔簇退化（环面秩至多为一）在Chow群层面的算术几何结构，特别是N乘法映射与Fourier变换的相互作用，为深入研究退化情形的motive理论提供基础。

Method: 通过分析环面秩至多为一的主极化阿贝尔簇退化上的有理N乘法映射在Chow群上的作用，结合Fourier变换的交互性质，推导单位截面的类，建立相对Chow motive的广义权分解，并利用这些工具完全刻画tautological环。

Result: 计算了单位截面的类；证明了通用退化族相对Chow motive满足广义权分解；完整确定了其tautological环的结构。

Conclusion: 该工作将阿贝尔簇乘法映射与Fourier变换的理论推广到环面秩至多为一的退化情形，为退化阿贝尔簇的motive理论提供了完整的结构描述。

Abstract: We study the action of the rational multiplication by $N$ map on the Chow groups of degenerations of principally polarized abelian varieties of torus rank at most one, as well as its interaction with the Fourier transform. As applications, we compute the class of the unit section, prove a generalized weight decomposition of the relative Chow motive of the universal family of such degenerations, and completely determine its tautological ring.

</details>


### [130] [Semi-abelian reduction of Albanese varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14417)
*Tai-Hsuan Chung*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明：若射影几何正规簇X_K（定义在DVR分式域上）存在一个射影模型，其特殊纤维在余维数一上至多具有结点（nodes），则其阿尔巴尼斯簇Alb_{X_K/K}在同一基上具有半阿贝尔约化。作为应用，将Fontaine和Abrashkin关于Z上阿贝尔概形的经典不存在定理推广到奇异情形——例如，若平坦射影Z-概形X具有正规且几何连通的纤维，则H^1(X_Q, O_{X_Q})=0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Fontaine-Abrashkin定理表明不存在非平凡阿贝尔概形定义在Z上（即Z上的阿贝尔概形均为平凡的）。本文旨在将该结果推广到奇异射影簇的情形，为此需要建立奇异簇阿尔巴尼斯簇的半阿贝尔约化性质，作为推广该不存在定理的关键技术工具。

Method: 本文首先研究射影几何正规簇X_K的阿尔巴尼斯簇Alb_{X_K/K}。在假设其存在一个射影模型且特殊纤维在余维数一上至多具有结点（即奇点余维数≥2）的条件下，利用该类模型的某种稳定性/紧化性质来论证Alb_{X_K/K}具有半阿贝尔约化。随后将此结果应用于构造Z上平坦射影概形的论证，从而推广Fontaine-Abrashkin定理至奇异情形。

Result: 得到两个主要结果：（1）在上述结点条件下，阿尔巴尼斯簇具有半阿贝尔约化；（2）作为推论，若平坦射影Z-概形X具有正规且几何连通的纤维，则H^1(X_Q, O_{X_Q})=0，这推广了Fontaine-Abrashkin不存在定理到奇异射影簇。

Conclusion: 本文成功将Fontaine-Abrashkin定理从光滑阿贝尔概形情形推广到具有某些较弱奇点条件（特殊纤维在余维数一上仅含结点）的奇异射影簇，核心桥梁是建立了这类奇异簇阿尔巴尼斯簇的半阿贝尔约化定理。

Abstract: Let X_K be a projective geometrically normal variety over the fraction field of a DVR. We show that if X_K admits a projective model whose special fibre has at worst nodes in codimension one, then the Albanese variety Alb_{X_K/K} has semi-abelian reduction over the same base. As an application, we extend the classical nonexistence theorem of Fontaine and Abrashkin for abelian schemes over Z to the singular setting. For instance, if a flat projective Z-scheme X has normal and geometrically connected fibres, then H^1(X_Q, O_{X_Q})=0.

</details>


### [131] [Classifying Smooth Quot Schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14424)
*Roy Skjelnes,Gregory G. Smith,Michael Stillman*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了n维射影空间上秩r平凡向量丛的Quot概形，找出了决定其光滑性与不可约性的完整数值条件，并揭示了字典序点的光滑性等几何特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Quot概形是模空间理论中的基本对象，但关于其何时光滑且不可约的问题长期缺乏系统性回答。作者希望通过Hilbert多项式q的数值条件给出完全刻画。

Method: 通过分析Hilbert多项式q的数值性质，建立判断Quot概形光滑性与不可约性的充要条件，并利用字典序单模（lexicographic point）的结构进一步分析该概形的几何性质。

Result: 给出了q上完全决定Quot概形光滑与不可约的数值条件，并发现了字典序点的光滑性等额外的几何性质。

Conclusion: 对给定秩r和维数n的射影空间上Quot概形的光滑性与不可约性给出了完整的数值刻画，深化了对该模空间几何结构的理解。

Abstract: The Quot scheme $\operatorname{Quot}^{q}(\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^n}^{r})$ parametrizes the quotients of the trivial vector bundle of rank $r$ on $n$-dimensional projective space that have Hilbert polynomial $q$ and are flat over a base scheme. We identify numerical conditions on the polynomial $q$ that completely determine when this Quot scheme is smooth and irreducible. Our approach also uncovers further geometric features of the projective scheme $\operatorname{Quot}^{q} (\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^n}^{r})$ including the smoothness of the lexicographic point.

</details>


### [132] [Effective Bialynicki-Birula-Brosnan motivic decompositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14485)
*Charles De Clercq*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Brosnan关于各向同性约化群G的齐性簇动机分解定理的SageMath实现，该实现基于Weyl群的Cayley图深度优先搜索，算法复杂度与动机的大小相关而非整个Weyl群。作为应用，作者提供了若干例外群的新的动机分解，并将Karpenko对典型群的分解推广到特征2的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Brosnan定理给出了内型各向同性约化群G的射影齐性簇X的动机可以分解为各向异性核的射影齐性簇动机的Tate扭转的直接和，但该分解的实际计算在理论上依赖于Weyl群的完整结构，计算成本高昂。需要一个高效的算法实现来实际计算这些分解，并用于探索例外群等此前未解决的实例。

Method: 作者基于Bialynicki-Birula、Hesselink和Iversen的结果以及Brosnan的分解定理，设计了一种SageMath实现。核心算法是对G的Weyl群的Cayley图进行深度优先搜索（DFS），使得计算复杂度与所涉及的动机大小成正比，而非与整个Weyl群的大小成正比，从而显著降低计算成本。

Result: 该实现为若干例外群提供了新的动机分解结果，并将Karpenko关于典型群的动机分解延拓到了特征2的情形，验证了算法的有效性和广泛适用性。

Conclusion: 该SageMath实现提供了一种实用且高效的工具来计算各向同性约化群的齐性簇动机分解，不仅填补了现有理论结果的算法空白，还通过新分解的发现展示了其在代数几何动机理论中的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Let $G$ be an isotropic reductive group and $X$ be a projective $G$-homogeneous variety. Using results from Bialynicki-Birula, Hesselink and Iversen, Brosnan showed that if $G$ is of inner type, the motive of $X$ can be expressed as a direct sum of Tate twists of motives of projective homogeneous varieties for the anisotropic kernel of $G$. We provide a SageMath implementation of this decomposition, based on a depth-first search of the Cayley graph of the Weyl group of $G$, ensuring the complexity scales with the size of the motive rather than the full Weyl group. As applications, we provide new motivic decompositions for some exceptional groups and show how to extend Karpenko's decompositions for classical groups to characteristic $2$.

</details>


### [133] [A remark on the full support property](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14540)
*Yiran Cheng*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明对射影概形上的数值预稳定性条件，质量-Hom界蕴含关于完整数值Grothendieck群的支持性质。结合近期构建的稳定性条件，在任意域上射影概形上得到具有完整支持性质的稳定性条件，并推广到相对情形及建立典范分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 支持性质是稳定性条件理论中的关键概念，但此前在一般射影概形上，关于完整数值Grothendieck群的支持性质尚未被完全建立。该论文旨在通过质量-Hom界这一较弱的条件，推导出完整支持性质，从而统一并简化已有结果。

Method: 论文首先证明质量-Hom界蕴含支持性质的关键定理，然后将其与近期关于射影概形上稳定性条件的存在性构造相结合，直接推导出完整支持性质。同时作者将论证推广到相对射影情形以建立均匀版本，并通过对极化无关性的分析，证明完整数值稳定流形中的区分分量不依赖于具体极化的选择。

Result: 成功在任意域上任意射影概形上建立了具有完整支持性质的稳定性条件；在相对情形下建立了均匀版本；证明了完整数值稳定流形中区分分量与极化选择无关，定义了典范分量。

Conclusion: 质量-Hom界是获得完整支持性质的充分条件，且该结果在相对情形下有均匀版本；由此在一般射影概形上得到最完整的稳定性条件理论，并揭示了稳定流形结构的典范性。

Abstract: We show that a mass-Hom bound for a numerical pre-stability condition on a projective scheme over a field implies the support property with respect to the full numerical Grothendieck group. Combined with the recent construction of stability conditions on projective schemes, this yields stability conditions with full support property on every projective scheme over a field. We also establish a uniform version in the relative setting. Finally, we show that the distinguished component in the full numerical stability manifold is independent of the choice of polarization, thereby defining a canonical component.

</details>


### [134] [Compressive Domains and a Bound for the Number of Components of the Fixed Locus of a Self-Map of the Berkovich Line](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14545)
*Xander Faber,Niladri Patra*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文引入了有理函数在Berkovich射影直线上的"压缩域"概念，证明了该压缩域必含经典不动点，并据此给出了有理函数不动点集连通分支数的最优上界。对多项式情形提供了第二个证明，使用了Rivera-Letelier的未发表质量公式。最后给出了压缩域内关键权重关于经典不动点数和边界点数的显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有理函数在Berkovich射影直线上的动力学行为，特别是不动点集的结构。压缩域的概念为理解不动点集的连通性提供了一种新的几何框架，从而可以获得更精确的不动点集结构估计。

Method: 首先定义有理函数在Berkovich射影直线上作用的压缩域概念，证明压缩域必含经典不动点。利用这一事实导出不动点集连通分支数的最优上界。对于多项式函数情形，应用Rivera-Letelier的质量公式给出第二证明，最后通过组合分析推导压缩域内关键权重的显式公式。

Result: 证明了压缩域必包含经典不动点；给出了有理函数不动点集连通分支数的尖锐上界；为多项式情形提供了基于Rivera-Letelier质量公式的替代证明；得到了压缩域内权重依赖于经典不动点数和边界点数的显式公式。

Conclusion: 压缩域是研究非阿基米德动力学中不动点结构的有效工具，其结果不仅给出了不动点集连通性上界的精细刻画，还通过显式权重公式揭示了不动点的分布规律，为非阿基米德域上的有理映射动力学提供了新的理论支撑。

Abstract: We introduce the notion of a "compressive domain" for the action of a rational function on the Berkovich projective line over a complete nontrivially-valued algebraically closed nonarchimedean field. We prove that such a domain always contains a classical fixed point, and we leverage this fact to give a sharp upper bound for the number of connected components of the fixed locus of a rational function. We give a second proof for polynomial functions that uses a previously unpublished mass formula of Rivera-Letelier. Finally, we give an explicit formula for the crucial weight inside a compressive domain as a function of the number of classical fixed points and boundary points.

</details>
